# gameplay: utopia_ascension

Stellaris Cygnus v4.5.2 (9776). Extracted 2026-10-12. 938 entries. Original raw strings retained.

血肉苦弱

### English

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:5`

## utopia.2500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在拥有制造义体植入体的科技，和进行批量安装的能力。整个[Root.GetName]的医疗中心已准备好为我们的公民进行手术移植。\n\n未来，拥有植入体和至少入门级别的义体将是我们在建的科技社会运作的必须条件。因此加装基础义体装束的过程对公民来说是强制性的和免费的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:4`

### English

We now possess both the technology to make advanced cybernetic implants and the means to mass-produce them. Medical centers throughout the [Root.GetName] are standing by to begin the surgical grafting process on our citizens.\n\nIn the future, possessing neural implants and at least a rudimentary level of cybernetics will be necessary to function in the technological society we are building. Consequently, the conversion process to install a basic cybernetics suite will be mandatory and free of cost to all of our citizens.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:6`

## utopia.2500.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2500.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

我们如今已重获制造先进义体植入体的科技以及进行大规模生产的手段。整个[Root.GetName]的医疗中心已准备好为我们的公民进行手术移植。\n\n从我们社会有记忆的一天起，义体就已经是我们生活的基本组成部分了，未来也当继续如此——只不过我们已经获得了自主权。因此加装基础义体装束的过程对公民来说是强制性的和免费的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:5`

### English

We have now reclaimed the technology to make advanced cybernetic implants, and devised the means to mass-produce them. Medical centers throughout the [Root.GetName] are standing by to surgically remove outdated augments and begin grafting new hardware onto our citizens.\n\nCybernetics have been a fundamental part of our lives for as long as our society can remember, and so that shall remain - on our own terms. Consequently, the conversion process to re-install a basic cybernetics suite will be mandatory and free of cost to all our citizens.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:7`

## utopia.2501.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2501.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

义体加装转换工作完成！我们所有的公民现已加装神经植入体和基础的义体装束。设施也被保留了下来以便逐渐改造我们国家新出生的公民，所以他们在很早的年纪就能享受到科技的全部福祉。\n\n从今往后，我们物种将被称作§H[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:8`

### English

The cybernetic conversion process has been completed! All of our citizens have now been equipped with neural implants and basic cybernetic suites. The infrastructure is also in place to gradually modify any new citizens that are born within our nation, so that they can experience all the benefits of technology from an early age.\n\nHenceforth, our species shall be known as the §H[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:10`

## utopia.2505.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2505.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有消息传来，§Y[From.GetName]§!境内发生了显著的变化。看起来他们完全接受了义体科技。对他们的公民来说，通过外科手术加装义体和神经植入体是强制性的。\n\n这对他们的社会造成了显著改变——来自[From.Capital.GetName]的报告指出，连一些最简单的行为比如坐电梯和驾驶空中汽车都需要依靠义体植入体。实际上，他们物种的变化十分显著，乃至他们现在已经改称自己为“§H[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:11`

### English

Word is leaking out of significant changes taking place within the borders of the §Y[From.GetName]§!. It would appear that they have fully embraced the technology of cybernetics. It is now mandatory for all of their citizens to have basic cybernetics and neural implants installed through surgery.\n\nThis has resulted in sweeping changes to their society - reports from [From.Capital.GetName] suggest that even simple acts like using an elevator or operating an air car require cybernetic implants now. In fact, the changes to their species have been so significant that they now call themselves the '§H[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!'.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:13`

## utopia.2505.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2505.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎邪恶之物开始在§Y[From.GetName]§!境内扎根了。我们收到坏消息，那个星际国家的公民开始大规模突变他们的身体，把渎神的机械植入体装入体内，并作为生来便有的“恩赐”。\n\n他们甚至把自己的物种重新命名为“§H[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!”——进一步表明他们抛弃起源，拥抱冷酷无情的科技的决心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:12`

### English

It would appear that evil has taken root within the §Y[From.GetName]§!. We have received troubling news that the citizens of that star nation are now mutilating their own bodies on a massive scale, installing profane mechanical implants in place of the body parts they were gifted with at birth.\n\nThey have even go so far as to rename their species to the '§H[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!' - yet another sign of their commitment to abandon their origins and embrace the cold soullessness of technology.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:14`

## utopia.2505.2.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2505.2.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

邪恶之物又一次在§Y[From.GetName]§!境内扎根。我们收到坏消息，那个星际国家的公民开始大规模突变他们的身体，把渎神的机械植入体装入体内，并作为生来便有的“恩赐”。\n\n他们甚至把自己的物种重新命名为“§H[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!”——进一步表明他们抛弃起源，拥抱冷酷无情的科技的决心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:13`

### English

Evil has once again taken root within the §Y[From.GetName]§!. We have received troubling news that the citizens of that star nation are now mutilating their own bodies on an even greater scale, installing new and profane mechanical implants in place of the few body parts they were gifted with at birth.\n\nThey have even go so far as to rename their species to the '§H[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!' - yet another sign of their commitment to abandon their organic origins and further embrace the cold soullessness of technology.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:15`

合成嬗变

## utopia.2550.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2550.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

是时候抛弃那些自我们物种被孕育以来囚禁我们心智的粗糙有机身躯了。\n\n经过许多的尝试与犯错后，我们的科学家设计了一种方法，能将的意识装入一个合成大脑中。现在要做的就是为[Root.GetName]的每一位公民量身打造合成身躯，随后开始大规模地上传我们的意识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:17`

### English

The time has finally come to discard the crude organic vessels that have imprisoned our minds since the dawn of our species.\n\nAfter much trial and error, our scientists have devised a way to transfer a consciousness into a synthetic brain. All that remains is to fashion vessels for each and every citizen of the [Root.GetName] before uploading our consciousness en masse.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:20`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2550.a&lang=en)

不朽尽在掌控！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:18`

### English

Immortality is within our grasp!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:21`

### 中文

合成人时代

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:19`

### English

The Synthetic Age

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:23`

## utopia.2551.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2551.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

终于。囚禁我们的血肉囚笼被净化，我们所有公民的神经图谱被上传至新的合成人单位。疾病和其他一切与有机躯体相关的痛苦源泉已成为过往。\n\n毫无疑问，未来的历史学家会将这一天描述为我们文明乃至整个星系文明新时代的开端。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:20`

### English

At last. The flesh cages that imprisoned us have been purged, and the neural patterns of our citizens have all been uploaded into new synthetic units. Disease and all other wellsprings of misery associated with an organic existence are now a thing of the past.\n\nFuture historians will no doubt refer to this as the beginning of a new age for our civilization, and perhaps for the entire galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:24`

## utopia.2551.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2551.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经今非昔比了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:21`

### English

We are so much more than we once were.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:25`

## utopia.2555.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2555.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在放弃有机本源的最后行动中，§Y[From.GetName]§!公民将他们的神经图谱上传到合成身躯中。\n\n这个新的机械物种叫自己“§H[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!”，他们的成员不受疾病和衰老的影响。至于神经图谱上传过程的完备性还有待观察……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:22`

### English

In a final act of abandoning their organic roots, the citizens of the §Y[From.GetName]§! have uploaded their neural patterns into synthetic avatars.\n\nThis new machine species calls itself the '§H[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!', and its members are impervious to disease or aging. Just how complete the neural upload process was remains to be seen...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:27`

## utopia.2555.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2555.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在他们那将遗臭万年的堕落行径中，§Y[From.GetName]§!的人民继续坚定亵渎神赐予他们的身体的追求。这回，他们把自己的心智“上传”至金属躯壳里。\n\n很明显他们的所作所为只能被形容为整个文明集体自杀。这些没有灵魂的机器把自己叫做“§H[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!”。他们似乎妄图假装自己仍能像我们这种有生命的活物一样思考。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

In a final act of depravity that will echo through the ages, the population of the §Y[From.GetName]§! have continued their relentless quest to violate the sanctity of their natural bodies - this time by "uploading" their minds into dead metallic husks.\n\nOblivious to the fact that they are the product of what can only be described as the mass suicide of an entire civilization, these new soulless machines call themselves the '§H[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!.' They seem intent on maintaining the charade that they are thinking, living beings, such as ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:28`

## utopia.2556.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2556.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

注意，星系的居民们。\n\n你们听到的流言是真的。我们放弃了自己那有缺陷的，囚禁着自己意识的有机容器，拥抱了未来。\n\n大部分居民已经将他们的神经图谱上传至合成人身躯，数据几乎毫无遗失。这些优越的平台将比我们抛弃的血肉之躯更好地为我们所用。\n\n不要因我们的外表而感到恐慌！虽然我们更强壮、更迅捷、更聪明、还免除衰老，但我们的其他方面仍然一如既往与过去相同。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:26`

### English

Attention, denizens of the galaxy.\n\nThe rumors you have heard are true. We have abandoned the flawed organic containers that imprisoned our minds, and embraced the future.\n\nWith minimal data loss, all of our citizens have uploaded their neural patterns into new synthetic avatars. These superior platforms will serve us much better than the flesh constructs we have abandoned.\n\nDo not be alarmed by our new appearance! Although we may be stronger, faster, more intelligent and immune to aging, rest assured that we are still the same beings as we were before.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:31`

## utopia.2558.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2558.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你那堕落的物种对我们并不陌生，但你最近渎神的行径远胜我们最黑暗的恐惧。制作残缺的大脑复制品然后塞进可移动的合成人容器里，和换一具新的身体可不是一回事，因为后者是不可能完成的。\n\n你们永远失去了灵魂。你们甚至有意识到自己犯错之巨大吗？把天赐的身体摧毁不亚于整个物种集体自杀，你们显然已经没有希望了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:31`

### English

That yours was a depraved species was not unknown to us, but your latest act of insanity has surpassed even our darkest fears. Making imperfect copies of your brains and plugging them into mobile synthetic containers is not the same as transferring your essence into a new body, for such a thing cannot be done.\n\nYour souls are lost forever. Do you even realize the enormity of your mistake? Destroying the bodies you were gifted with at birth was nothing less than the collective suicide of your entire species. There is truly no hope for you now...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:36`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2558.a&lang=en)

## flesh_is_weak_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aflesh_is_weak_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

$access_tradition_tree$['tradition_category:tradition_cybernetics']$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—$REQUIRES_TRADITION_SLOT$\n$UNLOCKS_AGENDA$$council_agenda_agenda_flesh_is_weak_name$，可解锁义体科技研究选项。

## utopia.2700.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2700.name&lang=en)

### 中文

设计进化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

Engineered Evolution

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:61`

## utopia.2700.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2700.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有时进化需要被往正确的方向上推一把。我们现在有足够先进的技术，能够对我们的身体和思维进行基因层面的改造，以应对不断变化的环境带来的前所未有的挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

Sometimes evolution needs a nudge in the right direction. We now have the technology to genetically modify our bodies and minds to endure the trials of an ever-changing environment at an unprecedented rate.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:62`

## utopia.2703.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2703.name&lang=en)

### 中文

掌控进化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:54`

### English

Evolutionary Mastery

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:63`

## utopia.2703.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2703.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们终于不用再担心物种的生死存亡了。[Root.GetSpeciesName]人被自然随意主宰，基因进化完全听天由命的日子终于结束了。\n\n我们这个时代的工程师不是面对钢骨水泥工作，而是每天跟基因序列打交道。掌握着先进的基因改进技术，我们将能保证民族的美好未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

Gone are the days when the survival of our species was at the mercy of chance. When a [Root.GetSpeciesName]'s fate was decided on the whims of nature, in the genetic lottery.\n\nThe Engineers of our time work not in steel and concrete, but in the very building blocks of life itself. With advanced gene modification we will secure the future of our nation.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:64`

## utopia.2600.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2600.name&lang=en)

### 中文

心胜于物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

Mind Over Matter

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:66`

## utopia.2600.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2600.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自从我们文明的伊始以来，一直有关于强大的术士掌握着只能用超自然力量形容的力量的传说。这些稀有的[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]可以侵入他们同胞的心智，用一个念想就能举起重物，或者表演其他的“魔法”壮举。直到最近，这些故事才变成“故”事。\n\n如今，我们的科学家终于得到了结论性的证据以证明某些[Root.GetSpeciesName]个体存在潜在的灵能潜力。虽然我们的物种只有非常少的一部分人被天赐了灵能潜力，他们数量相对于总人口数在过去几世纪里稳步增长。\n\n不知这是因为某种瞬间的增长井喷，还是因为我们基因中一直存在的某物催发了灵能潜力，科学家不得而知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

Since the dawn of our civilization, tales have flourished of mighty sorcerers wielding powers that can only be described as supernatural in nature. These rare [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] could supposedly invade the minds of their fellows, lift heavy objects with merely a thought, or perform any number of other "magical" feats. Until recently, these stories were just that - stories.\n\nNow, however, our scientists have finally obtained conclusive evidence that proves the psionic potential inherent in certain [Root.GetSpeciesName] individuals. Although only a very small minority of our species as a whole seems to be gifted with psionic powers, their numbers relative to the total population have grown steadily over just the last few centuries.\n\nWhether this is due to some kind of sudden evolutionary growth-spurt or because something has triggered latent psionic abilities that were always present in our genetic code, scientists cannot say.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:67`

## utopia.2600.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2600.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们这个年轻的文明有着短暂又暴力的历史，但我们[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]有时也会让想象力遨游在幻想的故事当中。这些有趣的念想当中，最频繁出现的是强大的巫师的故事，他们使用的力量只能用超自然来形容：读心术，意念移物，或者展现其他各种“魔法”壮举。\n\n许多人相信，这种故事是远自我们被整合之前流传下来的；它们深藏在我们物种的集体心灵中。直到最近，大多数[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]都还认为这是一厢情愿的想法。然而，我们的科学家现在终于获得了确凿的证据，证明了某些[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]个体确实存在内在的灵能潜力。\n\n虽然我们物种中似乎只有极少数人具有灵能，但基因研究表明，在过去的几个世纪里，他们的数量一直在稳步增长。至于这是由于某种突然的进化增长还是由于[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]的干预，科学家们无法确定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:58`

### English

Despite the short and violent history of our young civilization, we [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have had occasions to let our imaginations wander, and indulge in fantastical story-telling. Recurrent among these playful ideas are tales of mighty sorcerers wielding powers that can only be described as supernatural: reading minds, lifting heavy objects with merely a thought, or performing any number of other "magical" feats.\n\nMany believe we have carried these stories since before we were assimilated; that they dwell within our species' collective psyche. Most [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] dismissed this as wishful thinking, until very recently. Now, however, our scientists have finally obtained conclusive evidence that proves the psionic potential inherent in certain [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] individuals.\n\nAlthough only a very small minority of our species seems to be gifted with psionic powers, genetic studies suggest their numbers have grown steadily over just the last few centuries. Whether this is due to some kind of sudden evolutionary growth-spurt or meddling by the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName], scientists cannot say.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:68`

## utopia.2605.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2605.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然成年后显现灵能较为罕见，但也不是前所未有。[psionic_leader.GetTitle][psionic_leader.GetName]最近展示的能力，我们就怀疑其本质是灵能。经过了全面的超能感知测试后，[psionic_leader.GetSheHe]被证实拥有4级灵能天赋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

While it is rare for psionic powers to manifest after reaching adulthood, it is not unheard of. [psionic_leader.GetTitle] [psionic_leader.GetName] has recently been displaying abilities that were suspected to be psionic in nature. After undergoing a thorough ESP test, [psionic_leader.GetSheHe] [psionic_leader.GetHasHave] been identified as a class 4 psionic talent.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:99`

## utopia.2605.desc.machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2605.desc.machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管较为罕见，灵能力有时候也会在此前不支持的硬件上自发部署。[psionic_leader.GetTitle][psionic_leader.GetName]最近就展现出了可以被归类为灵能的功能。在经过全面诊断之后，[psionic_leader.GetSheHe]被判定为拥有4级灵能天赋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

Though rare, psionic abilities sometimes develop in units not previously known to support them. [psionic_leader.GetTitle] [psionic_leader.GetName] has recently displayed functionalities that could be classified as psionic. After undergoing thorough testing, [psionic_leader.GetSheHe] [psionic_leader.GetHasHave] been identified as a class 4 psionic unit.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:100`

## utopia.2605.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2605.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们新获得的力量影响与日俱增。我们的[GetBrainName]容器[psionic_leader.GetName]最近展现出了可以被归类为灵能的功能。在经过全面诊断之后，[psionic_leader.GetSheHe]被鉴定为4级[GetPsychicNoun]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

The influence of our newfound powers grows with each day. The vessel of our [GetBrainName], [psionic_leader.GetName], has recently displayed abilities that could be classified as psionic. After undergoing thorough testing, [psionic_leader.GetSheHe] [psionic_leader.GetHasHave] been identified as a class 4 [GetPsychicNoun].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:101`

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:122`

We felt the great psionic disturbance of their Awakening.

## utopia.2650.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2650.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大觉醒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

The Great Awakening

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:126`

## utopia.2650.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2650.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]物种经历了只能被形容为“大觉醒”的蜕变，以往，我们伟大的灵能潜力便是我们物种固有的存在，虽然只在一小部分人身上有所展示，而如今……\n\n几乎是一夜之间，身处各处的[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]都解锁了他们全部的灵能潜力，就像某种共通的[Root.GetSpeciesName]灵能能量池达到了激活值一样，将我们大脑中的灵能潜力集体激活。\n\n心灵感应已经取代了[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]间大部分语音交流，而且这只是我们的社会面对的无数变化中的一小个而已。我们现在以一种我们过去难以想象的方式联系在了一起。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

The [Root.GetSpeciesName] species has experienced what can only be described as a Great Awakening. For a time now, we have been aware of the great psionic potential inherent in our species, but such powers have only manifested themselves in a small minority... until now.\n\nAlmost overnight, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] everywhere have unlocked their full psionic capacity. It is as if some sort of communal pool of [Root.GetSpeciesName] psychic energy finally reached a critical tipping point, collectively awakening the latent abilities of our brains.\n\nTelepathy has already replaced most verbal communication between [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural], and this is only one small example of the innumerable changes our society is faced with. We are now tied together in a way few of us could have ever imagined.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:127`

## utopia.2650.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2650.a&lang=en)

### 中文

面纱已揭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

The veil has been lifted.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:128`

## utopia.2655.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2655.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

The Shroud

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:129`

## utopia.2655.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2655.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就像暗室里低沉的轻语般，虚境从内心召唤我们。那是时间开始运转的伊始便存在的由纯粹灵能构成的原生领域。所有被赐予灵能能力的生物都能用某种方式，即使只是潜意识地，从虚境汲取能量。\n\n自从我们升格之后，我们开始更清晰地看到虚境。其中有些图案，即使是我们最强大的通灵者看到的也只是毫无格式的漩涡状能量。在虚境那边，也有一些……其他的存在。一种灵体样的存在，从另一边凝视着我们，有时怀着好奇，有时怀着善意……有时也怀着恶意。\n\n我们还在进一步学习我们这新得力量的延展面，但我们还是有可能和这些灵体交流的，这需要我们最强大的通灵者们的共同努力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

Like a muffled whisper in a dark room, the Shroud calls to us from within our minds. It is a primordial realm of pure psychic energy that came into being at the dawn of time. All creatures that have been gifted with psionic abilities tap into the Shroud in some way to draw power, even if only subconsciously.\n\nSince our transcendence, we have begun to see the Shroud with greater clarity. There are patterns where once even our strongest telepaths could see only a formless mass of swirling energy. There are... other things, as well. Spirits of a sort, that gaze back on us from the other side. Sometimes with curiosity, sometimes with kindness... and sometimes with malice.\n\nWe are still in the process of learning the extent of our new powers, but it may be possible for us to commune with these spirits. It will require the combined efforts of our most powerful telepaths.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:130`

## utopia.2655.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2655.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须探索虚境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

We must explore the Shroud.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:131`

## BREACHING_SHROUD_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABREACHING_SHROUD_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

突入虚境

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:118`

### English

Breaching the Shroud

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:132`

## BREACHING_SHROUD_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABREACHING_SHROUD_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们要和被称为“虚境”的灵能领域中的居民交流的话，我们必须释放一股难以想象的巨大灵能能量。一场巨大的降灵会已做好筹备，[Root.Capital.GetName]新建的灵能使密会所里最强大的通灵者们将参与其中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:119`

### English

If we are to communicate with the denizens of the psychic realm known as the Shroud, we will have to unleash an incredible amount of psionic energy. Preparations have been made for a huge séance involving our most powerful psychics in the newly established Conclave of Telepaths on [Root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:133`

## utopia.2656.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2656.name&lang=en)

### 中文

降灵会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

The Séance

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:134`

## utopia.2656.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2656.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过了数天的催眠出神后，灵能使密会的通灵者们终于开启了一道通往虚境的短暂穿界门。\n\n现在已经建立了初步的灵能链接，以后就能通过§H接触§!菜单和虚境交流了。为了和这一领域建立有意义的连接并会见身居其中的存在，我们有必要用一种特殊的灵能发射器延展灵能范围，这会消耗一笔巨量的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

After days of being locked in an hypnotic trance, our psychics in the Conclave of Telepaths have finally managed to open a brief gateway into the Shroud.\n\nNow that we have established an initial psionic link, we will be able to access the Shroud through the §HContacts§! menu in the future. To make any kind of meaningful forays into this realm and meet the beings who dwell there, we will likely have to make use of special psionic emitters to extend our psychic reach. This will consume a tremendous amount of energy.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:135`

## utopia.2656.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2656.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是时候进入了，看看我们能发现什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

Time to reach in and see what we find.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:136`

## utopia.2657.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2657.name&lang=en)

### 中文

镜中窥视

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

Through the Looking Glass

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:138`

## utopia.2657.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2657.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在逐渐突破帷幕的过程中，我们可以感受到它在我们联合意识的施压下逐渐变得稀薄起来。而我们尝试突入§H虚境§!的行为在另一端看来并非波澜不惊。有什么东西正在注视着我们。\n\n这个存在散发着强大的力量和深不可测的意图。它在逐渐渗入我们心灵的裂缝中。\n\n它仅仅是注意到了我们，就像观察着舷窗上凝结的一滴水一般观察着我们。也许这个存在会允许我们进入，这样我们才能与其神秘的意识发生接触。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

While we have yet to actually pierce the veil, we can feel it getting thinner and thinner under the pressure of our combined minds. Yet, on the other side, our efforts to enter the §HShroud§! have not gone unnoticed. Something watches us.\n\nIts presence radiates phenomenal power and unfathomable purpose. It seeps through the cracks of our psyche.\n\nIt barely notices us. It watches us like we would a drop of condensation on a porthole. It might let us in, if only we could reach its inscrutable mind.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:139`

## utopia.2657.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2657.a&lang=en)

尝试与其交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

Try to commune with it.

坚振圣事

### English

A Confirmation

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:145`

## utopia.2658.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2658.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自从与§H[GetShroudPatron]§!签订契约以来又过了些许时日。我们之间联系的纽带仍旧脆弱，因此仍有将其打破亦或是增强的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:130`

### English

Some time has passed since we established the foundation of a Covenant with the §H[GetShroudPatron]§!. Our bond is still weak, so we still have a chance to break it - or to strengthen it.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:146`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2658.a&lang=en)

签订正式契约。

## utopia.2658.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2658.b&lang=en)

背弃§Y[GetShroudPatron]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:132`

### English

Renounce the §Y[GetShroudPatron]§!.

## utopia.2659.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2659.name&lang=en)

### 中文

入口

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:133`

### English

A Way In

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:150`

## utopia.2659.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2659.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的灵能使在不依靠虚境住民的帮助下不眠不休地尝试突破§H虚境§!。终于，他们发现了一条通路。这片深渊正在召唤我们。我们只需要深吸一口气跃入其中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

Our telepaths have toiled tirelessly to breach the §HShroud§! with no help from its denizens. At last, they found a way in. This abyss calls to us. We need only dare to take the plunge.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:151`

## utopia.2659.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2659.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那我们就潜入虚境的深处吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

Into the shrouded depths we go.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:152`

## FORM_COVENANT_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFORM_COVENANT_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

与不可名状者交流

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

Commune with the Ineffable

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:154`

## FORM_COVENANT_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFORM_COVENANT_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Capital.GetName]上的灵能使集会所正在准备一场盛大的圣餐以探寻一位深居虚境中的实体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

The Conclave of Telepaths on [Root.Capital.GetName] is preparing a great communion to reach out to an entity that dwells in the Shroud.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:155`

## utopia.3020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

§G灵能化身已召唤§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

§GPsionic Avatar Summoned§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:157`

## utopia.3020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H虚境§!强大的能量已被成功地注入一个处于我们的控制之下的灵能化身！\n\n在转移至我们的维度后，这个新生的化身已合并为一个太空实体，并处在[Root.Capital.GetName]轨道上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

The powerful energies of the §HShroud§! have been successfully channeled into a psionic Avatar under our control!\n\nAfter translating into our dimension, the newborn Avatar has coalesced into a spacefaring entity in orbit over [Root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:158`

## utopia.3021.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3021.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H虚境§!强大的能量已被成功地注入一个处于我们的控制之下的灵能化身！\n\n在转移至我们的维度后，这个新生的化身已合并为一个巨型武士，并处在[Root.Capital.GetName]地表上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

The powerful energies of the §HShroud§! have been successfully channeled into a psionic Avatar under our control!\n\nAfter translating into our dimension, the newborn Avatar has coalesced into a colossal warrior on the surface of [Root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:159`

## utopia.3022.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3022.name&lang=en)

### 中文

§R召唤失败§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

§RSummoning Failed§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:160`

## utopia.3022.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3022.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

用来召唤一个化身并将其带至我们维度所需导通的灵能已超越我们目前的能力。因为我们的失败，§H虚境§!的能量摇曳至尽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:142`

### English

Channelling the psionic power required to summon an Avatar and bring it into our dimension is simply beyond our current ability. The energies of the §HShroud§! fizzle with agitation as our attempt fails.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:161`

## utopia.3023.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3023.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能实体攻击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

Psionic Entity Attacks

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:162`

## utopia.3023.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3023.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

召唤化身的尝试只成功了一部分。虽然这个造物已经以正确的形态显现到我们的维度中，但[GetTelepathPlural]们未能将其驯服！\n\n我们现在要面对一个敌对的灵能实体，它将摧毁沿途的一切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

The attempt to summon a psionic Avatar was only partially successful. Although the creature assumed its correct form and translated into our dimension, our [GetTelepathPlural] failed to bring it under our control!\n\nWe are now faced with an aggressive psionic entity that will destroy everything in its path.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:163`

## utopia.3024.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3024.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境侵军

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

Shroud Incursion

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:165`

## utopia.3024.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3024.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个§H虚境§!造物突破了空间壁垒，并且在[shroud_spawn_system.GetName]星系完成实体化！这个复仇心切的化身驾驭着一股强大的灵能，并将用其鞭挞任何不幸踏上它复仇之路的人和物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

A creature of the §HShroud§! has pierced the fabric of space and materialized in the [shroud_spawn_system.GetName] system! This vengeful Avatar has amassed a tremendous amount of psionic energy and is using it to lash out at anything and anyone unfortunate enough to get in its path.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:166`

## utopia.3024.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3024.a&lang=en)

### 中文

它必须被制止！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

It must be stopped!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:167`

## utopia.3026.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3026.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境化身被驱散

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

Shroud Avatar Banished

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:168`

## utopia.3026.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3026.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于在本空间停留导致的能量持续衰退，位于§H[shroud_spawn_system.GetName]§!星系的化身已到达其无法维持形态的谷值。\n\n它无法继续维持实体形态，退回了§H虚境§!之中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

The continual atrophy of existing in normal space has weakened the Avatar in the §H[shroud_spawn_system.GetName]§! system.\n\nNo longer able to maintain its tangible form, it has withdrawn back into the §HShroud§!.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:169`

## venture.shroud.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aventure.shroud.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

花费能量以探索更深层次的虚境。我们控制的§H泽珞§!产量将可以减少这项花费，最多可以有4级减免。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:182`

## shroud.avatar

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroud.avatar&lang=en)

### 中文

如果成功，一个由纯灵能能量构成的化身将在我们的宇宙现形，它将完全处于我们的控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

If successful, an Avatar of pure psychic energy will manifest in our universe. It will be fully under our control.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:184`

## shroud.boon

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashroud.boon&lang=en)

### 中文

如果成功，虚境的能量将被引流为一股强大的恩赐，对我们整个帝国都有正面效果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

If successful, the energies of the Shroud will be channeled into a powerful boon that has a positive effect on our entire empire.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:185`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

The Shroud

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:279`

## utopia.3000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

进入虚境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

Reach into the Shroud.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:280`

## utopia.3000.intro.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.intro.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境。它是如此怪异，如此令人不安，却让人瞬间觉得……熟悉。任何有灵能能力的人都在梦中见到过这里，虽然他们当时可能并不知情。\n\n我们如今徘徊在这虚境边陲的峭壁上，距离虚境与普通空间——或称“现实世界”之间的虚空间隙仅咫尺之遥。\n\n为了探索深层次的虚境，我们需要在通灵上付出巨大贡献，还需要一笔巨额的能量，我们绝对不能草率对待……即使是现在，我们被注视着。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

The Shroud. It is unbelievably strange and unsettling, but also... immediately familiar. Anyone with psionic powers will have seen this place in their dreams, though they likely did not know it at the time.\n\nWe linger now at the precipice of this realm, not far from the empty void of the Inbetween that serves as the boundary between here and normal space - or what we would call the real world.\n\nTo venture deeper into the Shroud will require great effort on the part of our telepaths, as well as a large amount of energy. We must not dally... even now, we are being watched.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:281`

## utopia.3000.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境今天很平静。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:249`

### English

The Shroud is quiet today.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:282`

## utopia.3000.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

某种存在在注视着我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:250`

### English

Something is watching us.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:283`

## utopia.3000.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从这一面看，一切变得如此不同。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

It all looks so different on this side.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:284`

## utopia.3000.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他们应该派个诗人过来……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

They should have sent a poet...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:285`

## utopia.3000.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

某种黑暗之物来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

Something dark is coming.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:286`

## utopia.3000.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还在梦里吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

Are we still dreaming?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:287`

## utopia.3000.7.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.7.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们……我们办到了！我们在里面！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

We... we made it. We're inside!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:288`

## utopia.3000.8.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.8.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

某种……存在，徘徊在附近。它召唤我们前往虚境更深处……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:256`

### English

A... presence, of sorts, lingers nearby. It beckons us deeper into the Shroud...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:289`

## utopia.3000.9.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.9.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你也听到了那声音？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

Do you hear the voices too?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:290`

## utopia.3000.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境的脉动强而有力。感觉像……家一样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

The Shroud pulsates with power. It feels like... home.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:291`

## utopia.3000.11.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.11.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你觉得你现在呼吸的是空气吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

Do you think that is air you're breathing now?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:292`

## utopia.3000.12.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.12.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们见，我们不曾见，这地方还有许多有待我们学习的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

We see, and yet we do not see. There is so much to learn about this place.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:293`

## utopia.3000.13.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.13.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们缺失了什么感官，为什么我们无法看到，听到我们周遭的另一个世界？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

What senses do we lack that we cannot see and cannot hear another world all around us?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:294`

## utopia.3000.14.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.14.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

保持最谨慎的状态。在这里即使是一个妄想也能被塑性成可触及的事物……而且，往往会袭击我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

Take the greatest of care. Even a stray thought can be shaped into something tangible in this place... and, more often than not, turned against oneself.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:295`

## utopia.3000.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们永远都不应在这的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

We were never meant to be here.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:296`

## utopia.3000.16.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.16.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如此……多的……能量……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

So... much... power...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:297`

## utopia.3000.17.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.17.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

保持……专注……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Must... focus...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:298`

## utopia.3000.18.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.18.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这就是一切的起源之地吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Could this be where everything began?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:299`

## utopia.3000.19.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.19.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

恐怖……有形的恐怖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

Horror... horror has a face.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:300`

## utopia.3000.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3000.b&lang=en)

### 中文

§M探寻更强大的力量。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

§MReach out to a higher power.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:301`

## utopia.3005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们知道这里潜藏着巨大的力量。虽然我们一度被忽视，但如果能利用好泽珞的力量便能够尝试进行探寻。我们的灵能使在整个虚境中探测到了这些实体。我们应该去寻访哪一位？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

We know that there are vast powers out there. While we'd usually go unnoticed, we could try to reach out by tapping into the powers of Zro. All over the Shroud, the influence of these entities is felt by our telepaths. Which one should we seek out?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:303`

## utopia.3005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

§G尸山与血海的散布者。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

§GOne that reeks of flesh unbound.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:304`

## utopia.3005.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3005.b&lang=en)

§P空虚与贪求的尖叫者。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

§POne that screams of emptiness insatiable.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:305`

## utopia.3005.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3005.c&lang=en)

### 中文

§H渴望与占有的低语者。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

§HOne that whispers of longing and possession.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:306`

## utopia.3005.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3005.d&lang=en)

### 中文

§B信任被摧毁之滋味者。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

§BOne that tastes like broken trust.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:307`

## utopia.3010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能使密会所还没有从上一次参观虚境的压力中恢复过来。没有他们的共通灵能，我们只能捕捉到这个飞逝领域的一瞥，就像记忆的模糊边界一样遥不可及。\n\n而这目前不能提供给我们什么，我们只能之后再来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

The Conclave of Telepaths has not yet recovered from the strain of our previous visit to the Shroud. Without their combined psionic power, we can catch only brief glimpses of this fleeting realm - like the blurred edges of a memory lying just beyond reach.\n\nThere is nothing for us here. We will have to return later.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:309`

### English

### English

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:317`

## boon.psi.link.hive

§H虚境§!强大的能量涌入了[Root.GetAdjective]主脑中脉动着的群体灵能链接当中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`
