## boon.ship.link

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aboon.ship.link&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[GetTelepathPlural]们设法让我们的舰船和§H虚境§!间建立了暂时的传导通路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

Our [GetTelepathPlural] have managed to establish a temporary conduit between the §HShroud§! and our starships.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:320`

## utopia.3030.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3030.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n这些能量在集体网络中环流，确保我们的子个体在执行任务时获得最佳的稳定性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nThese energies course throughout the collective network, ensuring optimum stability as our drones fulfill their purposes.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:322`

## utopia.3030.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3030.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n一种对平静的深远认识迅速传遍了[Root.GetName]，甚至没有灵能能力的人也受之影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nA profound sense of calm is spreading throughout the [Root.GetName], and affecting even those without psionic abilities.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:323`

## utopia.3030.desc.machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3030.desc.machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n一种深刻的运行平衡感迅速传遍了[Root.GetName]，看起来那些没有灵能部件的单元甚至也被影响了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:284`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nA profound operational equilibrium is spreading throughout the [Root.GetName], and affecting even those without psionic components.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:324`

## utopia.3031.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3031.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.ship.link$\n\n无论服役记录如何，我们的舰队损耗的迹象都消失了。因此我们的维护成本大大降低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

$boon.ship.link$\n\nOur fleets exhibit no signs of degradation regardless of their service record. As a result, our upkeep costs have been significantly decreased.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:325`

## utopia.3032.desc.non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3032.desc.non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们的[GetScientistPlural]之间已经建立起了心灵感应共享集体知识库，这将极大地提高他们的工作效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nA collective knowledge base that is shared telepathically between our [GetScientistPlural] has been established, which will greatly increase the efficiency of their work.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:326`

## utopia.3032.desc.organic_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3032.desc.organic_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]主脑内的科研专注节点正以绝佳的同步率工作中，提升了认知效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nOur research focused nodes within the [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] overmind are operating in perfect synchronicity, increasing our cognitive efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:327`

## utopia.3032.desc.machine_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3032.desc.machine_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n灵能渲染循环使我们的研究矩阵超越了最高效率，极大地提高了学习效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nPsionic rendering loops are allowing our research matrix to operate beyond maximum efficiency, vastly improving learning efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:328`

## utopia.3033.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3033.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.ship.link$\n\n我们的战舰乘员现在能用灵能来强化护盾系统了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

$boon.ship.link$\n\nThe crews of our warships can now augment the strength of our shield systems by using their psionic powers.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:329`

## utopia.3034.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3034.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.ship.link$\n\n通过使用灵能，我们的船员现在能强化武器系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

$boon.ship.link$\n\nUsing their psionic powers, our crew is now able to augment the strength of our weapon systems.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:330`

## utopia.3035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.ship.link$\n\n通过使用灵能，我们的船员现在能加快武器的射速。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

$boon.ship.link$\n\nUsing their psionic powers, our crew is now able to increase the firing rate of our weapon systems.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:331`

## utopia.3036.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3036.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n一种对团结的认知开始出现在我们居民的心智中，他们现在看起来更愿意通力合作，向着政府设立的目标前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nA new sense of solidarity has appeared in the hearts and minds of our citizens, making them more willing to work together towards the mutual goals established by our government.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:332`

## utopia.3036.desc.machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3036.desc.machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n一种对团结的认知开始出现在我们居民的处理器中，他们现在看起来更愿意通力合作，向着政府设立的目标前进。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nA new sense of solidarity has appeared in the processing cores of our citizens, making them more willing to work together towards the mutual goals established by our government.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:333`

## utopia.3036.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3036.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n这些能量在集体中留存，优化着我们子个体的效率，降低各种分散我们注意力的反常行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nThese energies persist throughout the collective, streamlining the efficiency of our drones, reducing any lingering deviancy that was otherwise distracting our focus.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:334`

## utopia.3037.desc.organic_non_genocidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3037.desc.organic_non_genocidal&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们的同理心比以往更强烈了，导致我们拥有更大的政治影响力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nOur empaths are stronger than ever before, resulting in greater political influence.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:335`

## utopia.3037.desc.machine_non_genocidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3037.desc.machine_non_genocidal&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们的外交单元正在以最高的共情效率运作中，带来了更强大的政治影响力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nOur diplomacy units are operating at maximum empathetic efficiency, resulting in greater political influence.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:336`

## utopia.3037.desc.genocidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3037.desc.genocidal&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们的意志以前所未有的力度施加到了整个星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nOur will is forced upon the galaxy in magnitude like never before.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:337`

## utopia.3038.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3038.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们感受到了空前的清晰与团结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nWe have never experienced such clarity and unity of purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:338`

## utopia.3038.desc.non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3038.desc.non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们感到了前所未有的团结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nWe have never felt a greater sense of unity.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:339`

## utopia.3039.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3039.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.ship.link$\n\n通过使用灵能，我们的船员现在能直接影响舰船的移动，使船面对敌方火力时闪避率更高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

$boon.ship.link$\n\nUsing their psionic powers, our crew is now able to directly affect the movement of our ships, increasing their evasiveness in the face of enemy fire.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:340`

## utopia.3040.desc.organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3040.desc.organic&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.ship.link$\n\n通过使用灵能，我们的战舰乘员现在能行使有限的战地维修，用他们的心智修补船体裂隙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

$boon.ship.link$\n\nUsing their psionic powers, our crew is now able to perform limited field repairs, mending hull fissures with their minds alone.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:341`

## utopia.3040.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3040.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.ship.link$\n\n通过使用灵能，我们的战舰乘员现在能行使有限的战地维修，用他们的神经处理器修补船体裂隙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

$boon.ship.link$\n\nUsing their psionic powers, our crew is now able to perform limited field repairs, mending hull fissures with their neuroprocessors alone.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:342`

## utopia.3041.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3041.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们的[GetTelepathPlural]已用他们的力量暂时减缓了我们最杰出领袖单元的衰老进程，使他们的预计报废日期延缓了数十年。可惜的是我们没有足够多的能量将这种效果赐予大量人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nOur [GetTelepathPlural] have used this power to momentarily improve functional performance among our most prominent leader units, delaying their estimated obsolescence date by decades. Alas, there was not enough psionic energy to bestow this effect upon the population at large.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:343`

## utopia.3041.desc.organic_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3041.desc.organic_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们的[GetTelepathPlural]已用他们的力量暂时减缓了我们最杰出领袖子个体的衰老进程，使他们的自然寿命增加了数十年。可惜的是我们没有足够多的能量将这种效果赐予大量人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:304`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nOur [GetTelepathPlural] have used this power to momentarily slow down the aging process for our most prominent leader drones, adding decades to their natural lives. Alas, there was not enough psionic energy to bestow this effect upon the population at large.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:344`

## utopia.3041.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3041.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们的[GetTelepathPlural]利用这份力量暂时降低了最杰出领袖的老化进程，延续了几十年的自然寿命。惜的是我们没有足够多的能量将这种效果赐予大量人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:305`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nOur [GetTelepathPlural] have used this power to momentarily slow down the aging process for our most prominent leaders, adding decades to their natural lives. Alas, there was not enough psionic energy to bestow this effect upon the population at large.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:345`

## utopia.3041.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3041.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

所有领袖获得§Y$leader_trait_shroud_age$§!特质，获得§G+40§!年领袖寿命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

All leaders gain the §Y$leader_trait_shroud_age$§! trait, granting §G+40§! years of leader lifespan.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:346`

## utopia.3042.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3042.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.ship.link$\n\n通过使用灵能，我们的船员现在能强化舰船推进器的强度，使舰船亚光速速度明显提高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

$boon.ship.link$\n\nUsing their psionic powers, our crew is now able to augment the strength of the thrusters on their ships, resulting in greatly increased sublight speed.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:347`

## utopia.3043.desc.organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3043.desc.organic&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n这股力量强化了我们士兵的心智，他们现在愿意勇闯战场上的任何危险。他们的团队精神无人能出其右。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nThis has fortified the minds of our soldiers, who are now willing to brave any dangers on the battlefield. Their morale is without equal.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:348`

## utopia.3043.desc.machine_or_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3043.desc.machine_or_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n这股力量强化了我们战斗无人机的目标达成度，优化了战场效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nThis has reinforced our combat drones' commitment to their purpose, streamlining their efficiency in the field.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:349`

## utopia.3044.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3044.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$boon.psi.link$\n\n我们现在能感知到比传统传感阵列探测距离更远处的舰船移动了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

$boon.psi.link$\n\nWe can now sense vessels as they move through space at a far greater distance than any conventional sensor array would be capable of.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:350`

## utopia.3045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

逐出虚境

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

Ejected from the Shroud

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:352`

## utopia.3045.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3045.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[GetTelepathPlural]用来连接虚境的传导通路自我垮解了，因为虚境的数个能量层级没有任何预兆便改变了。\n\n我们轻易地就被逐回了普通空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

The conduit that our [GetTelepathPlural] were using to connect to the Shroud collapsed on itself as several energy layers of the psychic realm suddenly shifted without warning.\n\nWe are being summarily ejected back into normal space.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:353`

## utopia.3045.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3045.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[GetTelepathPlural]在与虚境交流时被强烈的灵压压垮了。\n\n他们不能继续维持链接了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

Our [GetTelepathPlural] have been overwhelmed by the intense strain they were put under during our communion with the Shroud.\n\nThey can no longer maintain the link.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:354`

## utopia.3045.3.desc_organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3045.3.desc_organic&lang=en)

### 中文

起初很慢，然后越来越快，我们对虚境的攀附逐渐松脱。我们的[GetTelepathPlural]在虚境失去立足点，发出了痛苦的喊叫，然后我们被逐回了普通空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

Slowly at first, then faster and faster, our grasp on the Shroud begins to slip. Our [GetTelepathPlural] cry out in anguish as they lose their foothold and we are ejected back into normal space.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:355`

## utopia.3045.3.desc_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3045.3.desc_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

起初很慢，然后越来越快，我们对虚境的攀附逐渐松脱。我们的[GetTelepathPlural]在虚境失去立足点，开始发生故障，然后我们被逐回了普通空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Slowly at first, then faster and faster, our grasp on the Shroud begins to slip. Our [GetTelepathPlural] begin to malfunction as they lose their foothold and we are ejected back into normal space.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:356`

## curse.psi.link.organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurse.psi.link.organic&lang=en)

### 中文

一股强大的灵能反噬沿着我们与虚境建立的传导通路袭来，而且侵入了全体[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]共用的群体灵能链接。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

A powerful psychic backlash has bounced back through our conduit with the Shroud, and into the communal psionic link that is shared between all [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:358`

## curse.psi.link.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurse.psi.link.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

一股强大的神经反噬沿着我们与虚境建立的传导通路袭来，而且侵入了全体[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]共用的群体灵能链接。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

A powerful neural backlash has bounced back through our conduit with the Shroud, and into the communal psionic link that is shared between all [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:359`

## curse.psi.link

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurse.psi.link&lang=en)

### 中文

[GetCursePsiLink]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

[GetCursePsiLink]

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:360`

## curse.ship.link

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurse.ship.link&lang=en)

### 中文

一股强大的灵能反噬沿着我们与虚境建立的传导通路袭来，而且似乎影响了我们的舰船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

A powerful psionic backlash has bounced back through our conduit with the Shroud, and it seems to be affecting our ships.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:361`

## utopia.3050.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3050.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n这导致了[Root.GetName]集体内部的扰动，带来全帝国的不稳定性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThis has resulted in a disturbance within the [Root.GetName] collective, leading to empire-wide destabilization.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:363`

## utopia.3050.desc.non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3050.desc.non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n这似乎对我们居民的幸福度产生了深远影响，如今一股对忧郁的认知席卷了[Root.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:321`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThis has had a profound effect on the happiness of our citizens, and a sense of gloom now pervades throughout the [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:364`

## utopia.3051.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3051.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n产生的震波在整个集体中回荡，在子个体中引起异常行为，导致了棘手的异常情况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:322`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThe resulting shockwaves throughout the collective has resulted in a troubling emergence of deviant behavior among our drones.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:365`

## utopia.3051.desc.non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3051.desc.non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n这种负面能量席卷了我们公民的内心。很多人开始向往和我们政府不合的激进理念。这种影响甚至感染了无灵能居民！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThis negative energy has spread doubt within the minds of our citizens. Many are turning towards new radical ideas that are not in line with those of our government. The effect has even spread to non-psychic citizens!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:366`

## utopia.3052.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3052.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.ship.link$\n\n它们的装甲变得易碎起来，只能承受比以往更少的伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

$curse.ship.link$\n\nTheir armor has grown brittle, and can no longer sustain the punishment it once could.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:367`

## utopia.3053.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3053.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n对我们神经网络造成的压力对全[Root.GetName]的单位建设造成了可观的负面影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThe resulting strain on our neural network has led to a notable drop in unit construction across the [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:368`

## utopia.3053.desc.organic_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3053.desc.organic_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n对整个集体造成的痛苦感受对新子个体的产出能力造成了可观的负面影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThe resulting pain felt throughout the collective has caused a notable diminishment in our ability to produce new physical drones.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:369`

## utopia.3053.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3053.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n因为某种原因，这对全[Root.GetName]的生育率产生了损害。这甚至对没有天生灵能能力的物种产生了影响！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nFor some reason, this has had a detrimental effect on birth-rates throughout the [Root.GetName]. This has even affected members of races without any inherent psionic ability!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:370`

## utopia.3054.desc.machine_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3054.desc.machine_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n由此产生的灵能渲染反馈循环影响了我们科研矩阵的全效率运转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThe resulting psionic rendering feedback loops are blocking our research matrix from efficiently operating at their full capacity.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:371`

## utopia.3054.desc.machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3054.desc.machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n我们研究者的[GetMindPhysicalLoc]遭到了过载，这对他们专注工作有很大影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThe [GetMindPhysicalLoc] of our researchers have been overloaded, leaving them unable to efficiently focus on their workloads.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:372`

## utopia.3054.desc.organic_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3054.desc.organic_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n我们[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]主脑内的科研专注节点遭到了过载，导致我们无法高效处理新的发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nOur research focused nodes within the [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] collective are overloaded. Leaving us unable to process new discoveries efficiently.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:373`

## utopia.3054.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3054.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n我们研究者的[GetMindPhysicalLoc]遭到了蒙蔽，导致他们无法高效专注地进行工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThe [GetMindPhysicalLoc] of our researchers have been clouded, leaving them unable to efficiently focus on their workloads.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:374`

## utopia.3055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n我们积聚政治影响力的速率被明显降低了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThe rate at which we accumulate political influence has been greatly diminished.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:375`

## utopia.3056.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3056.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n曾经存在于我们公民心中对团结的认知涣散了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThe sense of unity that once existed between our citizens has been shattered.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:376`

## utopia.3056.desc.machine_or_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3056.desc.machine_or_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n曾经存在于我们[Root.GetName]中的对团结的认知涣散了，导致了大规模的不协调。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThe sense of unity that once existed throughout the [Root.GetName] has been shaken, leading to widespread miscoordination.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:377`

## utopia.3057.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3057.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.ship.link$\n\n我们的舰船移动时，似乎被某种不可见的力量拽回来，大幅降低了亚光速航行的速度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

$curse.ship.link$\n\nOur ships move as if held back by an invisible force. This has resulted in much slower sublight travel.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:378`

## utopia.3058.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3058.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n这种负面能量侵蚀了我们地面部队的士气，这期间他们在战场上的效率会更低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nThis negative energy has eroded the morale of our ground troops, who will be much less effective in battle for a time.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:379`

## utopia.3058.desc.machine_or_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3058.desc.machine_or_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$curse.psi.link$\n\n该能量涌造成的反馈回路妨碍了地面部队的运作效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

$curse.psi.link$\n\nFeedback loops caused by this energy surge have damaged the operational efficiency of our ground troops.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:380`

## utopia.3059.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3059.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一股强大的灵能反噬沿着我们与虚境建立的传导通路袭来，而且这似乎干扰了我们的星际传感器。\n\n从所有来源收到的传感器分辨率都大幅降低了，导致探测距离变短。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

A powerful psionic backlash has bounced back through our conduit with the Shroud, and it seems to be interfering with our interstellar sensors.\n\nThe sensor resolution from all sources has been greatly diminished, resulting in much shorter sensor ranges.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:381`

### 中文

灰烬残垣

### English

Fire and Ruin

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:446`

## utopia.3100.desc_prefix

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3100.desc_prefix&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Capital.GetName]……景象模糊且难以辨认，但毫无疑问，那是[Root.Capital.GetName]，我们的首都。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

[Root.Capital.GetName]... the vision is hazy and indistinct, but there can be no question - it is [Root.Capital.GetName], our capital. 

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:447`

## utopia.3100.desc_suffix

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3100.desc_suffix&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n§H“并不是无数之一，这是既定之事。如同恒星注定升起一般。此乃命运。”§!\n\n这幻景来得快去得也快，只留下纷扰灵能的瘴气经久不散。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

\n\n§HNOT ONE OF MANY. IT SHALL COME TO PASS. AS SURELY AS THE SUN RISES. IT IS YOUR DESTINY.§!\n\nThe vision ends as quickly as it began, leaving behind a miasma of swirling psionic energy.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:448`

## utopia.3100.desc_default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3100.desc_default&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3100.desc_prefix$在烈火与浓烟的的笼罩下，巨大的城市看起来陌生又熟悉。成千上万……不，上百万残尸横死街头，定睛一看都是[Root.GetSpeciesName]的尸体。腐烂的恶臭让人难以忍受。\n\n§H“你们的未来。”§!\n\n废墟上死寂一片。望向天空，轨道上巨大的物体，拖着烟迹，开始缓慢却不可阻止地降向星球。我们是在见证无数可能的未来之一吗？$utopia.3100.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

$utopia.3100.desc_prefix$The great cities look different yet familiar, even through the obscuring wall of flames and smoke. Thousands... no, millions of mutilated bodies litter the streets, all of them [Root.GetSpeciesName]. The stench of decay is unbearable.\n\n§HYOUR FUTURE.§!\n\nNothing living remains in the ruins. In the sky above, massive orbital installations, trailing smoke, have begun a slow and inevitable descent towards the planet. Are we witnessing one possible future out of many?$utopia.3100.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:449`

## utopia.3100.desc_hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3100.desc_hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3100.desc_prefix$在烈火与浓烟的的笼罩下，巨大的城市看起来陌生又熟悉。成千上万……不，上百万残尸横死街头，定睛一看都是[Root.GetSpeciesName]的尸体。腐烂的恶臭弥漫。\n\n§H“你们的未来。”§!\n\n废墟上死寂一片。望向天空，轨道上巨大的物体，拖着烟迹，开始缓慢却不可阻止地降向星球。我们是在见证无数可能的未来之一吗？$utopia.3100.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

$utopia.3100.desc_prefix$The great cities look different yet familiar, even through the obscuring wall of flames and smoke. Thousands... no, millions of mutilated bodies litter the streets, all of them [Root.GetSpeciesName]. The stench of decay is pervasive.\n\n§HYOUR FUTURE.§!\n\nNothing living remains in the ruins. In the sky above, massive orbital installations, trailing smoke, have begun a slow and inevitable descent towards the planet. Are we witnessing one possible future out of many?$utopia.3100.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:450`

## utopia.3100.desc_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3100.desc_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3100.desc_prefix$在烈火与浓烟的的笼罩下，巨大的城市看起来陌生但可辨认。成千上万……不，上百万被拆解的单元散落街头，它们全都是[Root.GetSpeciesName]。破败之景环绕着我们。\n\n§H“你们的未来。”§!\n\n废墟上死寂一片。望向天空，轨道上巨大的物体，拖着烟迹，开始缓慢却不可阻止地降向星球。我们是在见证无数可能的未来之一吗？$utopia.3100.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

$utopia.3100.desc_prefix$The major cities look different yet recognizable, even through the obscuring wall of flames and smoke. Thousands... no, millions of dismantled units litter the streets, all of them [Root.GetSpeciesName]. We are surrounded by destruction.\n\n§HYOUR FUTURE.§!\n\nNothing functional remains in the ruins. In the sky above, massive orbital installations, trailing smoke, have begun a slow and inevitable descent towards the planet. Are we receiving one possible future out of many?$utopia.3100.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:451`

## utopia.3110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境此处风暴呼啸。大量的原生灵能能量流经这里，有时会狂暴地产生半智慧灵体生物。\n\n这些困惑的灵体经常相互冲突，在战斗中释放出能残留数千年的庞大灵能。事实上，现在就有几场这样的决斗正在进行。\n\n我们应该谨慎前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

The Shroud is tempestuous here. As massive currents of raw psionic energy are funneled through, violent reactions give birth to semi-intelligent apparitions.\n\nThese confused spirits often clash with one another, unleashing phenomenal amounts of psychic power in battles that can last millennia. In fact, several such duels seem to be taking place right now.\n\nWe should proceed with caution.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:456`

## utopia.3120.name

### English

## utopia.3120.desc_suffix

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3120.desc_suffix&lang=en)

### 中文

有一位访客，——一个强大的灵能探测器，我们对铸造它的心灵完全一无所知，他们对虚境也是如此。\n\n当他们和我们的灵能回声彼此触及时，我们心中闪过一副飘渺的幻景：巨大的有机生物漂浮在厚厚的气态大气中，一群较小的动物相伴左右。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

Visitors, like us - a powerful psionic probe made by minds as utterly alien to us as they are to the Shroud.\n\nAs their psionic echo touches our own, a faint image briefly flickers within us: massive organic beings floating in a thick, gaseous atmosphere, attended by swarms of smaller creatures.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:458`

## utopia.3120.desc_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3120.desc_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

在虚境的深处，我们探测到了一个存在。$utopia.3120.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

Deeper within the Shroud, we detect a presence. $utopia.3120.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:459`

## utopia.3120.desc_default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3120.desc_default&lang=en)

### 中文

在虚境的深处，一个存在突然展露了自身。$utopia.3120.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

Deeper within the Shroud, a presence suddenly makes itself felt. $utopia.3120.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:460`

## utopia.3130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无尽沙漠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

Boundless Sands

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:461`

## utopia.3130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一处无尽的沙漠蔓延在我们面前。我们曾经来过这。\n\n§H“刹拉 库尔‘弥勒姆 祖耐克？”§!\n\n有什么从沙中浮现了出来。我们难以看到。\n\n§H“扎拉比，穆拉斯克。”§!\n\n话语像雷鸣般响亮，他们从未预料到说话者的出现。我们只能被迫回答。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

An endless desert stretches before us. We have been here before.\n\n§H"Shala'kul milem zunak?"§!\n\nSomething emerges from the sand. It is difficult to see.\n\n§H"Zharabi, melasuk."§!\n\nThe words are like thunder, spoken by a mouth for which they were never intended. We are compelled to answer.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:462`

## utopia.3130.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3130.a&lang=en)

刹什卡 马拉克。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

Shashka malak.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:463`

### English

Tirit nali dasumara!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:464`

## utopia.3140.desc_common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3140.desc_common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们存在的本质展现在我们面前。数千个像我们当前所处的星系被锁死在多元宇宙冲突的无尽循环中。\n\n它开始，终结，又再度开始。每一次，万亿的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

the nature of our existence is laid bare. A multiverse where thousands of galaxies like our own are locked in endless cycles of conflict.\n\nIt begins, it ends, and then it begins again. Each time with trillions of

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:467`

## utopia.3140.desc_suffix

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3140.desc_suffix&lang=en)

§H“正是如此。”§!一个声音低语着，§H“而且永应如此。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

§H"So it is,"§! a voice whispers, §H"and so it shall always be."§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:468`

## utopia.3140.desc_default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3140.desc_default&lang=en)

### 中文

突然，在痛苦的瞬间清晰中，$utopia.3140.desc_common$生命悬在天平之上。\n\n$utopia.3140.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

Suddenly, in a painful moment of clarity, $utopia.3140.desc_common$ lives hanging in the balance.\n\n$utopia.3140.desc_suffix$

突然，在倏然的清晰中，$utopia.3140.desc_common$变量悬在天平之上。\n\n$utopia.3140.desc_suffix$

## utopia.3150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失落之声

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

Voices of the Fallen

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:471`

## utopia.3150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们正从远处俯视我们的星系，远到有几十万光年。毫无征兆地，灵能流便将我们以难以置信的速度冲向无数恒星，来到了一片属于失落帝国，[fallen_empire.GetName]，的宇域。\n\n急速掠过恒星，随后是行星，终于视线定格在一座[fallen_empire.GetSpeciesName]城市。借着远超我们数代的反重力科技，无数尖塔冲向天际。灵能流将我们带到了其中一座尖塔，穿过一面又一面墙，最终我们来到了一间会议室之类的房间。两个[fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]在房间里，坐在舒适的零重力椅上，相互交流。\n\n他们并没有注意到我们的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

We are looking down upon our galaxy from far above, at a distance of what must be hundreds of thousands of lightyears. Without warning, the psionic current plunges us down towards the myriad stars at an incredible speed, into the region of space belonging to one of the Fallen Empires - the [fallen_empire.GetName].\n\nStars swish by, then planets, until the vision finally settles on a [fallen_empire.GetSpeciesName] city. Incredible spires rise towards the sky, aided by anti-grav technology generations beyond our own. The current takes us into such a spire, through wall after wall until we reach a large meeting room of sorts. It is occupied by two [fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural], floating comfortably in zero-gravity chairs while conversing with each other in soft voices.\n\nThey are oblivious to our presence.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:472`

## utopia.3150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y进入他们的意识找寻秘密。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

§YReach into their minds in search of secrets.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:473`

## utopia.3151.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3151.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这两个[fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]显然是某种显赫人物，而且在共商国是。缓慢但沉稳，画面侵入他们的同时他们说的外星语言逐渐被翻译为我们理解的话语。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

The [fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are clearly dignitaries of some kind, discussing matters of state. Slowly, surely, the vision encroaches upon them while their alien speech is gradually transformed into words that have meaning to us.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:478`

## utopia.3151.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3151.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

两个[fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的心地正在瓦解。就像盛开的花一样，他们的心智尽开。我们很容易就能侵入。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

The mind-glow of the two [fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural] is overwhelming. Like a blossoming flower, their minds open and are laid bare. It would be so easy to reach in.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:479`

## utopia.3151.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3151.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在那。科技秘密被从他们的心智中挖出，就像从未加密电脑上复制文件一样简单。[fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]一向看低的贼现在完全占据了他们的心智。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

There. Technological secrets plucked from the recesses of their minds, as easy as one would copy a file from an unlocked computer. The [fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural] remain ignorant of the theft that has taken place within their very minds.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:480`

## utopia.3151.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3151.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H“……简直是疯了，如果她认为人民愿意接受长久以来习惯的生活水平哪怕有一点暂时的下降，那……”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

§H"...is mad. If she thinks that the people would be willing to accept even a momentary lapse in the living standards that they have grown accustomed to, there is..."§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:481`

## utopia.3151.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3151.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H“……简直是耻辱。想象一下连年轻物种都开始怀疑我们是否能完全了解自己的科技了！我们必须……”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

§H"...is a disgrace. Imagine if the younger races were to even suspect that we no longer fully understand our own technology! We must..."§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:482`

## utopia.3151.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3151.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H“……变得越来越强大了。议会成员确信如果我们不采取行动……”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

§H"...are growing more and more powerful. Elements of the Council are convinced that unless we take action..."§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:483`

## utopia.3151.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3151.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H“……不在乎议会怎么说。上进者和我们相差了数千年，他们可怜的舰队对我们的威胁就像伽祖瑞安蚊能吸干一只海拉希火蜥蜴一般不切实际。根本没必要……”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

§H"...don't care what the Council says. The upstarts are still millennia behind us, and their miserable fleets can no more threaten us than a Gazurian gnat could consume a Hilaxi fire lizard. There is no need for..."§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:484`

## utopia.3151.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3151.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H“……仍认为没必要使用惩罚性措施，他们还年轻，缺乏关照，但我们曾经也是这样。他们还需要时间来……”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

§H"...still think punitive actions are uncalled for. They are young and careless, but so were we once upon a time. There is still time for them to..."§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:485`

## utopia.3151.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3151.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H“……说也许我们需要采取行动，在年轻物种成长到过于强大前铲除他们。我只能说我同意他，但议会觉得现在太迟了……”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

§H"...and that perhaps we should have taken steps to cull the younger races before they grew too powerful. I have to say that I agree with him, but the Council feels that it is too late now to..."§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:486`

## utopia.3152.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3152.name&lang=en)

### 中文

密语未闻

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

Secrets Unheard

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:487`

## utopia.3152.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3152.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这两个[fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]显然是某种显赫人物，而且在共商国是。缓慢但沉稳，画面侵入他们的同时他们说的外星语言逐渐被翻译为我们理解的话语。\n\n突然，这两个外星人默不作声。\n\n§H“你感觉到了吗？”\n\n“嗯，有什么在看着我们”§!\n\n他们巡视着房间，直到他们盯着我们。\n\n§H“别出心裁，一个灵能探机。”§!\n\n§H“看起来它源自[Root.GetSpeciesName]。快，激活传输屏障。§!”\n\n画面在干扰中中断了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:442`

### English

The [fallen_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are clearly dignitaries of some kind, discussing matters of state. Slowly, surely, the vision encroaches upon them while their alien speech is gradually transformed into words that have meaning to us.\n\nSuddenly, the aliens go quiet and very still.\n\n§H"Can you feel it?"\n\n"Yes. Someone is watching."§!\n\nThey search the room until both of them are staring directly at us.\n\n§H"Remarkable. A psionic probe."§!\n\n§H"It appears [Root.GetSpeciesName] in origin. Quickly, activate the transplanar shield.§!"\n\nThe vision ends abruptly.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:488`

## utopia.3153.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3153.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。\n\n别以为你们那试图侵入我们领袖心智的龌龊尝试没有被注意到。\n\n你们会付出代价的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural].\n\nDo not think that your loathsome attempt to invade the minds of our leaders has gone unnoticed.\n\nThere will be consequences.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:489`

## utopia.3153.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3153.a&lang=en)

### 中文

但……但听我们解释！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

B-but... we can explain!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:490`

## utopia.3153.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3153.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这一定有合乎逻辑的解释的……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

There is a perfectly logical explanation for this...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:491`

## utopia.3160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空闪烁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

Flicker in the Void

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:492`

## utopia.3160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一颗闪烁着的星星在虚境内逐渐物质化。它用难以置信的速度飞行着，有时它会显形为配置不明的外星舰船。然后它便消失在灵能云迹中，来的快去的也快。\n\n那会是什么？是什么能以物理形态进入虚境随后从普通空间的某处出现？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

A small, flickering star materializes inside the Shroud. It flies past at incredible speed, and for a second, it reveals itself as an alien ship of unknown configuration. Then it vanishes in a cloud of psionic energy as quickly as it appeared.\n\nCan it be? Is it possible to physically enter the Shroud and then emerge somewhere else entirely in normal space?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:493`

## utopia.3160.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3160.a&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y在这逗留一阵。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

§YLinger for a while.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:494`

## utopia.3161.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3161.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

又一颗闪烁着的星星突然出现，但这次我们准备好了。那确实是外星舰船，并不像我们星系所见的任何一种。当它飞过时，我们感受到了乘员的心地，他们的秘密是我们的了。\n\n灵能跃迁引擎，天才的设计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

Yet another flickering star suddenly appears, but this time we are ready. It is indeed an alien ship, unlike any that has ever been seen in our galaxy. As it flies past us, we feel the mind-glow of its crew, and their secrets are ours.\n\nA Psionic Jump Drive. How ingenious.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:495`

## utopia.3161.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3161.name&lang=en)

### 中文

§G灵能跃迁引擎§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

§GPsionic Jump Drives§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:496`

## utopia.3162.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3162.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

又一颗闪烁着的星星突然出现，但这次我们准备好了。那确实是外星舰船，并不像我们星系所见的任何一种。当它飞过时，我们试图混入其乘员的心地，但他们的智力比我们以往所见的个体更神秘更不同。\n\n现在我们不仅被从他们的舰船逐出，他们粗鲁的灵能排斥跟随我们离开了虚境！我们星舰的亚光速速度暂时降低了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

Yet another flickering star suddenly appears, but this time we are ready. It is indeed an alien ship, unlike any that has ever been seen in our galaxy. As it flies past us, we attempt to merge with the mind-glow of its crew, but their intellects are darker and more alien than any we have yet encountered.\n\nNot only are we repelled from their vessel, but the psionic feedback from their violent rejection has followed us out of the Shroud! The sub-light travel speed of our starships has been momentarily reduced.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:497`

## utopia.3170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

护盾灵体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

Shielded Entity

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:498`

## utopia.3170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3170.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在虚境中追随着一个纤弱的回声，我们发现了一个静坐在某种强大的灵能护盾后的灵体。\n\n§H“走开。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

Having followed a faint echo in the Shroud, we encounter a spirit that has ensconced itself behind some kind of powerful psionic shield.\n\n§HLEAVE.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:499`

## utopia.3170.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3170.a&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y尝试突破护盾。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

§YAttempt to penetrate the shield.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:500`

## utopia.3171.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3171.name&lang=en)

### 中文

§G灵能护盾§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

§GPsionic Shields§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:501`

## utopia.3171.desc_suffix

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3171.desc_suffix&lang=en)

### 中文

然而，对打破护盾的尝试让我们也学会了如何创造出相似的力场。如果这种技术可以加以精进，我们就能形成大到可以包裹整艘星际舰船的灵能护盾了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

however, as the act of breaching the shield taught us how to generate a similar force field. If the technique can be refined, we may be able to form psionic shields large enough to encompass entire starships.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:502`

## utopia.3171.desc_machine_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3171.desc_machine_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们来到虚境如此深的地方后并没有无功而返。在汇聚了集体的全部灵能力之后，我们在护盾上产生了能让自己够穿过的突破口。\n\n在护盾另一侧的存在远超我们目前的理解能力，严重地过载了我们的数个灵能处理器，$utopia.3171.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

We did not venture this deep into the Shroud only to be turned back now. After focusing our collective psionic capacities, we were able to create a breach in the shield large enough to momentarily peer through.\n\nWhat lay beyond the shield is beyond our current understanding, critically overwhelming several of our psionic processors, $utopia.3171.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:503`

## utopia.3171.desc_organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3171.desc_organic&lang=en)

### 中文

我们来到虚境如此深的地方后并没有无功而返。在将我们灵能使的全部灵能力汇聚起来以后，在护盾上终于造成了一个大到足以穿过的开口。\n\n我们不知道他们在盾的另一边看到了什么，但那足以让他们永久性精神失常。他们的牺牲并没有白费，$utopia.3171.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

We did not venture this deep into the Shroud only to be turned back now. After channeling all of their psionic might, our telepaths were able to form a breach in the shield large enough for some of them to peer through.\n\nWe do not know what those telepaths saw on the other side of the shield, but it was enough to permanently unhinge their minds. Their sacrifice was not in vain, $utopia.3171.desc_suffix$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:504`

## utopia.3172.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3172.name&lang=en)

### 中文

未破之盾

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

Shield Unbreached

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:505`

## utopia.3172.desc_machine_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3172.desc_machine_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[GetTelepathPlural]，在如此深入虚境以后已经负荷过载，无法再汇聚足够多的灵能来穿透护盾。在失败后我们得到了这份通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

Our [GetTelepathPlural], already overloaded from having ventured this deeply into the Shroud, failed to channel enough psionic strength to breach the shield. Our failure was met with the following communication.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:506`

## utopia.3172.desc_machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3172.desc_machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[GetTelepathPlural]，在如此深入虚境以后已经负荷过载，无法再汇聚足够多的灵能来穿透护盾。我们的失败似乎刺激到了这个灵体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

Our [GetTelepathPlural], already overloaded from having ventured this deeply into the Shroud, failed to channel enough psionic strength to breach the shield. The failed attempt seems to have provoked the spirit.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:507`

## utopia.3172.desc_organic_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3172.desc_organic_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[GetTelepathPlural]，在如此深入虚境以后已经疲惫不堪，无法再汇聚足够多的灵能来穿透护盾。在失败后我们得到了这份通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

Our [GetTelepathPlural], already exhausted from having ventured this deeply into the Shroud, failed to channel enough psionic strength to breach the shield. Our failure was met with the following communication.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:508`

## utopia.3172.desc_organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3172.desc_organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[GetTelepathPlural]，在如此深入虚境以后已经疲惫不堪，无法再汇聚足够多的灵能来穿透护盾。我们的失败似乎刺激到了这个灵体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

Our [GetTelepathPlural], already exhausted from having ventured this deeply into the Shroud, failed to channel enough psionic strength to breach the shield. The failed attempt seems to have provoked the spirit.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:509`

## utopia.3180.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3180.name&lang=en)

### 中文

等待未来

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

Awaiting the Future

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:510`

## utopia.3180.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3180.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

就我们所知，时间的概念对虚境的这片区域完全是外来物。在这看起来像是数个世纪般久的时间可能等同于真实世界的数小时或数分钟。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

The concept of time, as we know it, is alien to the Shroud. What can seem like centuries here may equal mere hours or even minutes in the real world.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:511`

## utopia.3180.desc_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3180.desc_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3180.desc.intro$只要我们知道如何观察的话，预知未来的能力便触手可及。\n\n这种能力对我们集体有很大的风险，我们要小心使用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

$utopia.3180.desc.intro$ If we know where to look, precognition may yet be within our grasp.\n\nSuch an ability comes with risks to our collective, if we proceed it must be with caution.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:512`

## utopia.3180.desc_machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3180.desc_machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3180.desc.intro$只要知道如何观察的话，在其中甚至可以一瞥未来。\n\n预知未来的概念对我们的灵能处理器有极大的过载风险，我们要小心使用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

$utopia.3180.desc.intro$ If one knows where to look, it is even possible to glimpse into the future...\n\nThe concept of precognition runs a heavy risk of overloading our psionic processors, we must proceed with caution.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:513`

## utopia.3180.desc_organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3180.desc_organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3180.desc.intro$只们知道如何观察的话，在其中甚至可以一瞥未来。\n\n对灵能使来说预知未来是最明确无误能导致疯狂的行为之一，我们要小心使用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

$utopia.3180.desc.intro$ If one knows where to look, it is even possible to glimpse into the future...\n\nPrecognition is one of the surest paths to madness for a telepath, so we must use caution.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:514`

## utopia.3180.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3180.a&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y在这冥想一会。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

§YMeditate here for a moment.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:515`

## utopia.3181.desc_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3181.desc_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

在避免单元过载的前提下只能维持有限的虚境预知链接，但概率数据的流量仍然过于庞大以至于难以投入实用。\n\n然而，我们的研究节点还是找到了这些数据的一项次要用途，就是将其应用在舰船上来提高战斗效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

A limited precognitive connection to the Shroud can be maintained without risking unit overload, but the massive influx of probability data is too overwhelming to be of much practical use.\n\nHowever, our research nodes have found minor applications for this data, applying it to our fleets to improve their combat efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:516`

## utopia.3181.desc_machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3181.desc_machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

在避免单元过载的前提下只能维持有限的虚境预知链接，但概率数据的流量仍然过于庞大以至于难以投入实用。\n\n然而，如果我们能制造出用来解析数据的数码界面，其将有可能被用于改善星舰的战斗电脑……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

A limited precognitive connection to the Shroud can be maintained without risking unit overload, but the massive influx of probability data is too overwhelming to be of much practical use.\n\nStill, if a digital interface could be devised to interpret this data, it could potentially be applied to improve the performance of our starships' combat computers...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:517`

## utopia.3181.desc_organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3181.desc_organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以维持和虚境建立有限的预知连接的同时避免陷入疯狂，但概率数据的巨大流量将压垮我们导致其很难有实际用处。\n\n然而，如果我们能设置一个数码界面以解析数据，其将有可能被用于改善星舰的战斗电脑……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

A limited precognitive connection to the Shroud can be maintained without risking insanity, but the massive influx of probability data is too overwhelming to be of much practical use.\n\nStill, if a digital interface could be devised to interpret this data, it could potentially be applied to improve the performance of our starships' combat computers...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:518`

## utopia.3181.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3181.name&lang=en)

### 中文

§G预知接口§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

§GPrecognition Interface§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:519`

## utopia.3182.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3182.name&lang=en)

### 中文

§R虚境癫狂症§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:474`

### English

§RShrouded Madness§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:520`

## utopia.3182.desc_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3182.desc_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

正如我们担心的，尝试理解这种同时存在于过去和未来的难以想象的多重可能性在我们的灵能工蜂中造成了大规模的神经紧张症。\n\n这使他们与主脑断绝了联系失去了指令。如果想再次探索达虚境，我们需要回收这些有缺陷的躯体，恢复集体的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:475`

### English

As feared, attempting to comprehend the inconceivable multitude of options and probabilities that exist both in the past and the future has resulted in large cases of catatonia in our psionic drones.\n\nThis has left them severed from the overmind and leaving them without purpose. If we wish to reach the Shroud again, we will need to recycle these defective husks and recover our collective strength.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:521`

## utopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.1&lang=en)

### 中文

这种同时存在于过去和未来的难以想象的多重可能性。\n\n每个变化和场景，无论它们间的差别有多巨大或是微小，在他们的[GetSpeciesBrainPlural]中重复闪现。他们现在陷入了永久的精神紧张状态。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:476`

### English

the inconceivable multitude of options and probabilities that exist both in the past and the future.\n\nEvery single variation and scenario, no matter how colossal or miniscule the difference between them, is being endlessly repeated inside their [GetSpeciesBrainPlural]. They have been reduced to a state of permanent catatonia.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:522`

## utopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.2&lang=en)

### 中文

新的[GetTelepathPlural]，我们还需要一段时间才能集中足够的灵能力量再次进入虚境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:477`

### English

 new [GetTelepathPlural], it will be some time before we can muster the psionic strength needed to reach into the Shroud again.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:523`

## utopia.3182.desc_machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3182.desc_machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

不出所料，我们的灵能处理器无法理解$utopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.1$由于需要建造$utopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:478`

### English

As we expected, our psionic processors were unable to comprehend $utopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.1$As we will need to construct$utopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.2$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:524`

## utopia.3182.desc_organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3182.desc_organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

正如我们担心的，通灵者们陷入了疯狂。同时间，他们的心智开放向$utopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.1$由于需要重新招募$utopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

As we feared, madness has overtaken our telepaths. All at once, their minds have been opened to $utopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.1$As we will need to recruit$utopia.3182.desc.non_gestalt.2$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:525`

## utopia.3190.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3190.name&lang=en)

### 中文

高密度能量

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

Concentrated Energy

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:526`

## utopia.3190.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3190.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在虚境中找到了一小处我们之前从未见过的巨量灵能能量聚集的空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

We have found a tiny space within the Shroud that has a concentration of psionic energy that is of a magnitude we have never before seen.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:527`

## utopia.3190.desc_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3190.desc_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3190.desc.intro$只要成功利用，这种灵能将会弥散到集体中而不会有什么后果。然而。然而我们也可以指定一个子个体来作为这份能量的直接通路，使其变得非常强大。\n\n目前尚不清楚其个体形式是否能够处理如此巨大的能量而不产生致命的后果。我们挑选了一些候选者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:482`

### English

$utopia.3190.desc.intro$ Such psionic energy, if successfully tapped, would dissipate amongst the collective without much consequence. However we may be able to designate a single 'chosen' drone to act as a direct conduit for this energy, becoming immensely powerful in the process. \n\nIt is not known if their solitary form will be able to handle such volumes of energy without fatal repercussions. We have compiled a selection of candidates.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:528`

## utopia.3190.desc_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3190.desc_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3190.desc.intro$任何[GetPsychicNoun]只要能成功潜入这片纯能量构成的区域就会变得无比强大……但这只够一个人使用，并且还涉及到一定的风险。\n\n整个[Root.GetName]只有极少人具有足够强大灵能，能进入池中汲取能量还不丢掉性命。该让谁前往呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:483`

### English

$utopia.3190.desc.intro$ Any [GetPsychicNoun] that managed to successfully tap into this pocket of raw energy would become immensely powerful... but there is only enough for a single individual, and there are risks involved.\n\nThere are only few individuals in the entire [Root.GetName] that possess the psionic strength needed to tap into this energy pocket with any hope of survival. Who should it be?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:529`

### English

[GetChosenName]
