## utopia.3191.desc_organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3191.desc_organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3191.desc_machine_non_gestalt$愿圣灵恩慈我们的灵魂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

$utopia.3191.desc_machine_non_gestalt$ May the Spirits have mercy on our souls.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:543`

## utopia.3191.desc_machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3191.desc_machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

完……完工了。当灵能能量池被抽干后整个虚境似乎都震颤了起来，其中的力量被传输给了[chosen_leader.GetName]。\n\n星系历史上只有屈指可数的个体拥有过如此强大的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

It... it is done. The entire Shroud seems to shudder as the psionic energy pocket is drained, and its power transferred to [chosen_leader.GetName].\n\nOnly a handful of individuals in the history of the entire galaxy has been gifted with psionic strength of this magnitude.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:544`

## utopia.3191.desc_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3191.desc_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功地将整个灵能口袋中的能量导入了选定的目标，[chosen_leader.GetName]体内。\n\n这些能量在我们的集体海洋中小如沙粒，[chosen_leader.GetName]可以将其作为强大的工具为[Root.GetName]的所有[Root.Species.GetName]谋利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

We have successfully funnelled the full power of the psionic energy pocket into our designated target, [chosen_leader.GetName].\n\nWhat would otherwise have been a drop in our collective ocean is able to serve as a powerful tool for the [Root.GetName] now that [chosen_leader.GetName] wields it for the benefit of all [Root.Species.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:545`

## utopia.3192.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3192.name&lang=en)

### 中文

§R领袖之死§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

§RDeath of a Leader§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:546`

## utopia.3192.desc_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3192.desc_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

当池中巨量的能量传输给[chosen_leader.GetName]时，整个虚境似乎都在震颤。\n\n然而如我们所料，这种力量已经超过了任何单一实体所能承受的范围。[chosen_leader.GetName]的物理形态化为了爆炸性的灵能波，他的声音被从[GetBrainName]中抹除了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

The entire Shroud shudders as the immense psionic energies contained within the pocket are channeled into [chosen_leader.GetName].\n\nHowever, as suspected, such power is beyond the means of one singular entity. The physical form of [chosen_leader.GetName] has erupted into an explosive burst of psionic energy, separating their voice from the [GetBrainName].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:547`

## utopia.3192.desc_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3192.desc_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

当池中巨量的能量传输给[chosen_leader.GetName]时，整个虚境似乎都在震颤。\n\n不幸的是，[chosen_leader.GetHerHis]的[GetSpeciesBrain]似乎无法承受这压力。也许没有活物能拥有这样的力量。[chosen_leader.GetName]被吞噬并引发灵能大爆炸，一并杀死了周边的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

The entire Shroud seems to shudder as the immense psionic energies contained within the pocket are transferred to [chosen_leader.GetName].\n\nUnfortunately, [chosen_leader.GetHerHis] [GetSpeciesBrain] was unable to cope with the stress. Perhaps such power cannot be contained by physical entities. [chosen_leader.GetName] was consumed in a large explosion of psionic energy that killed all who were nearby.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:548`

## utopia.3193.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3193.name&lang=en)

### 中文

§R灵能变形§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

§RPsionic Transformation§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:549`

## utopia.3193.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3193.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

当池中巨量的能量传输给[chosen_leader.GetName]时，整个虚境似乎都在震颤。\n\n虽然传输成功了，但是[chosen_leader.GetName]……发生了变化……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

The entire Shroud seems to shudder as the immense psionic energies contained within the pocket are transferred to [chosen_leader.GetName].\n\nAlthough the transfer was successful, [chosen_leader.GetName] has been... transformed...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:550`

## utopia.3193.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3193.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n§H“下等孽畜……你们……你们对我……做了……什么……”§!\n\n§H“你们……都将……付出……狂妄的代价……”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

\n\n§HINSIGNIFICANT WHELPS... WHAT... WHAT HAVE YOU... DONE... TO ME... §!\n\n§HYOU... WILL... PAY... FOR SUCH HUBRIS...§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:551`

## utopia.3193.desc_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3193.desc_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3193.desc.intro$他从集体中脱离，一个完全陌生的实体夺舍了他的身躯。$utopia.3193.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

$utopia.3193.desc.intro$ severing them from the collective as an entirely foreign entity inhabits and possesses their form.$utopia.3193.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:552`

## utopia.3193.desc_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3193.desc_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3193.desc.intro$他的身体被某种纯能量的存在夺舍了。$utopia.3193.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

$utopia.3193.desc.intro$ their body inhabited and possessed by a construct of pure energy.$utopia.3193.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:553`

## utopia.3200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.name&lang=en)

幻象

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

A Vision

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:555`

## utopia.3200.1.desc_organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.1.desc_organic&lang=en)

### 中文

这片虚空闪烁着能量，让我们休息一会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

The void here shimmers with power. Let us rest a while.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:556`

## utopia.3200.1.desc_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.1.desc_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

这片虚空闪烁着能量，让我们充能一会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

The void here shimmers with power. Let us recharge for a while.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:557`

## utopia.3200.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们前进的道路突然遇到了尽头，我们不能再前进一步了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

The path we followed has come to an abrupt end. We can go no farther.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:558`

## utopia.3200.3.desc_organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.3.desc_organic&lang=en)

### 中文

有什么在和我们说话，某种奇妙的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Something speaks to us here. Something wonderful.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:559`

## utopia.3200.3.desc_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.3.desc_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

有什么在和我们说话，某种值得关注的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

Something speaks to us here. Something remarkable.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:560`

## utopia.3200.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

因为某种我们未知的原因，我们被牵拉到了虚境中的这个能量矿脉。但为什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

For reasons we cannot fathom, we have been drawn toward this vein of energy in the Shroud. Why?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:561`

## utopia.3200.5.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.5.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

席卷了我们，仿佛在虚境中深思了一个永恒。随后我们重新找到立足点，恐惧也逐渐消散。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

washes over us as we contemplate an eternity spent within the Shroud. Then we regain our footing, and the terror recedes.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:562`

## utopia.3200.5.desc_organic

## utopia.3200.6.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.6.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们走向虚境深处，一群灵体围绕着我们舞蹈良久。他们闪耀着寻常空间中从未见过的炫目色彩。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

As we venture deeper into the Shroud, a flock of spirits dance around us for several hours. They glow with dazzling colors that we have never perceived in normal space.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:565`

## utopia.3200.6.desc_organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.6.desc_organic&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3200.6.desc.intro$这种奇景令人沉醉，直到我们突然意识到：我们正受其驱使。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

$utopia.3200.6.desc.intro$The spectacle is enjoyable, until the realization hits us: we are being herded.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:566`

## utopia.3200.6.desc_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.6.desc_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3200.6.desc.intro$这种奇景令人惊奇，直到我们突然意识到：我们正受其驱使。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

$utopia.3200.6.desc.intro$The spectacle is fascinating, until the realization hits us: we are being herded.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:567`

## utopia.3200.7.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.7.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一块无瑕的水晶碎片漂浮在这，顺着它的旋转轴缓慢自转着。当我们接近时，它碎裂成了数千块碎片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

A shard of perfect crystal floats here, rotating slowly around its own axis. As we approach, it shatters into a thousand pieces.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:568`

## utopia.3200.8.desc_organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.8.desc_organic&lang=en)

### 中文

一具[Root.GetSpeciesName]的遗体横躺在前面。腐化的尸体在爆出一阵刺耳的尖叫后消散了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

The corpse of a dead [Root.GetSpeciesName] tumbles through the void ahead. The putrid carcass emits a shrill screech before it vanishes out of sight.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:569`

## utopia.3200.8.desc_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.8.desc_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

一台[Root.GetSpeciesName]的空壳横躺在前面。锈蚀的外壳在爆出一阵刺耳的尖叫后消散了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

The shell of a broken [Root.GetSpeciesName] tumbles through the void ahead. The ravaged husk emits a shrill screech before it vanishes out of sight.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:570`

## utopia.3200.9.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3200.9.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不敢确定这是不是现实之事。我们究竟是真的在虚境中，或

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

We cannot be certain this is really happening. Are we truly in the Shroud, or

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:571`

## utopia.3200.9.desc_hive

## MESSAGE_TERRAFORM_FOUND_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_TERRAFORM_FOUND_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]被识别为一颗环境改造候选星球。我们会将这颗[From.GetPlanetMoon]作为未来殖民目标进行追踪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

[From.GetName] has been identified as a Terraforming Candidate. The [From.GetPlanetMoon] will be tracked as a potential future colonization target.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:579`

## anomaly.2575.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2575.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现环境改造候选

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Terraforming Candidate Discovered

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:580`

## anomaly.2575.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2575.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[From.GetName]的详尽扫描表明这个星球在遥远的过去维持过生命。两极冰盖和[From.GetPlanetMoon]地表下明显蕴藏着冰冻水。干枯的河谷网的存在说明水曾经在星球表面自由流动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

Our detailed survey of [From.GetName] has revealed that it may once have supported life in the distant past. There are significant deposits of frozen water at the polar ice caps and beneath the [From.GetPlanetMoon]'s surface. The existence of valley networks suggests that the water may once have flowed freely.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:581`

## anomaly.2575.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2575.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.2575.desc.intro$\n\n对[From.GetPlanetMoon]环境改造理论上是可行的，但我们目前所拥有的科技并不足以在切合实际的时间内完成这一壮举。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

$anomaly.2575.desc.intro$\n\nTerraforming this [From.GetPlanetMoon] would theoretically be possible, but we do not yet possess the technology to accomplish this monumental task within a realistic timeframe.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:582`

## anomaly.2575.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2575.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.2575.desc.intro$\n\n能量花费将令人生畏，但我们拥有将[From.GetPlanetMoon]逐渐环境改造成再次能维持生命的状态所必须的科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

$anomaly.2575.desc.intro$\n\nThe energy costs would be daunting, but we do have the technology to gradually terraform this [From.GetPlanetMoon] into something that would support life once again.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:583`

## anomaly.2575.desc.3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2575.desc.3&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.2575.desc.intro$\n\n很不幸，这一恒星系中的§Y戴森球§!使得恢复行星环境不再可能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

$anomaly.2575.desc.intro$\n\nUnfortunately the §YDyson Sphere§! in this system has removed any possibility of restoring the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:584`

## anomaly.2575.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.2575.a&lang=en)

### 中文

总有一天它能被再度唤醒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

In time, perhaps we can reawaken it.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:585`

## utopia.3300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境住民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

A Presence in the Shroud

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:587`

## utopia.3300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这里有一个灵体，占据了虚境的这一整片区域。我们曾经见过灵体，但这个不一样，这个力量远超其他……这个更加古老。\n\n他似乎没有察觉我们的存在。也许他在沉睡，也许我们不足以引起他的注意。\n\n也许我们应该离开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

There is a presence here, occupying this entire part of the Shroud. We have met spirits before, but this is something else, something vastly more powerful... something far more ancient.\n\nIt seems unaware of us as of yet. Perhaps it is sleeping. Perhaps we are simply beneath its notice.\n\nPerhaps we should leave.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:588`

### English

## utopia.3301.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3301.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们接触到这个存在时，我们感受到祂内心的波动，祂的注意力现在全在我们身上。看起来祂不喜欢被打扰。\n\n一股能量鞭挞我们的通灵者，对一些通灵者造成了极大的痛苦并让其他人失去了意识。我们没有选择，只能暂且从虚境中撤出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

As we reach out for the presence, we can feel it stirring, its attention now fully on us. It does not seem to appreciate being disturbed.\n\nA wave of energy lashes at our telepaths, causing great agony to some and knocking others unconscious. We have no choice but to withdraw from the Shroud for the time being.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:594`

## utopia.3302.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3302.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们接触到那个存在时，我们感受到祂内心的波动，祂的注意力现在全在我们身上。看起来祂不喜欢被打扰。\n\n一股庞大的灵能袭向我们，我们的灵媒全部失去了意识。当他们恢复过来时，发现有一个人失踪了。§Y[leader_to_kill.GetName]§!不见踪影，[leader_to_kill.GetSheHe]曾经站立的地方只有一道灵能造成的小裂缝留下的痕迹，就好像那个实体到达我们的世界把[leader_to_kill.GetHerHim]拖入虚境一样，为了……算了，谁知道到底是为了什么。\n\n不管祂为什么夺走了[leader_to_kill.GetHerHim]，毫无疑问我们再也见不到[leader_to_kill.GetName]了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

As we reach out for the presence, we can feel it stirring, its attention now fully on us. It does not seem to appreciate being disturbed.\n\nAn enormous wave of psionic energy crashes against us, blacking out the senses of our telepaths. When they come to, they notice one of their number is missing. §Y[leader_to_kill.GetName]§! is nowhere to be found, and where [leader_to_kill.GetSheHe] [leader_to_kill.GetWasWere] standing there is now only the psionic trace of a small rift, as though the entity reached out into our world and dragged [leader_to_kill.GetHerHim] into the Shroud for... well, who knows what reason or purpose, really.\n\nNo matter its reasons for taking [leader_to_kill.GetHerHim], it seems doubtful that we will ever see [leader_to_kill.GetName] again.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:595`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3304.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们通灵者的内心响起了一阵齐声。最开始只是轻柔的低语，随后变为强烈的刺耳声，一千道声音开始齐声讲述。\n\n这些声音自称$whisperers_in_the_void_color_article$。合唱中的每个个体都感应到：秘密的低语、

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

the minds of our telepaths are filled with a chorus of voices. No more than soft whispers at first, the cacophony grows in intensity, a thousand voices speaking in unison.\n\nThe voices introduce themselves as $whisperers_in_the_void_color_article$. Individual members of the chorus make themselves known: the Susurrus of Secrets, the

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:599`

## utopia.3304.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3304.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

、人偶的大师，有许多许多的存在都加入了低语的合唱。祂——祂们——宣称通晓所有凡人向另一个凡人低语的秘密知识，并且愿意和我们分享这些知识……\n\n§H只要我们同意让祂们进入我们的意识。§!\n\n一个微不足道的代价，就像祂们宣称的那样。我们甚至难以察觉祂们的存在。\n\n祂们的语气中透露着急切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

, the Master of Puppets - there are so many of them, all joined in the chorus of the Whisperers. It - they - claim to be privy to all knowledge whispered in secrecy from one mortal to another, and offer to share some of these secrets with us...\n\n§HIf we will only allow them into our consciousness.§!\n\nA trifling price, they assure us. We will barely even notice their presence.\n\nThere is an urgency to their tone.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:602`

## utopia.3305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个存在和蔼亲切地欢迎了我们。祂不用语言而是幻景、概念和想法进行表达。祂渗入我们通灵者的内心，如此温和顺畅，以至于我们觉得祂仿佛本就在此一般。一连串的影像在他们面前一闪而过。\n\n这个存在被称为$the_composer_of_strands_color_article$，祂对我们的有机形态有强烈的好奇心和兴趣。祂向我们展示了一系列让身体生长、改变、提升、进化的方法。\n\n§H只要我们将祂带入我们的血肉。§!\n\n祂亲切而耐心地等待着我们的答复，就像父母宠溺地等待孩子一样。在祂的身后静卧着祂的孪生子，抟土之塑师。祂对我们的有机意识毫不关心。祂渴望创造意识，在电路里铸造灵魂。\n\n织缕等待着我们的答复。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

The presence welcomes us with genial familiarity. It speaks not so much in words but in visions, concepts, ideas. It seeps into the minds of our telepaths, so gently and smoothly that one might think it had always been there. A flurry of images flash before them.\n\nThe presence is known as $the_composer_of_strands_color_article$, and it regards our organic forms with curious interest. It shows us a variety of ways in which they can be grown, changed. Improved. Evolved.\n\n§HIf we will only take it into our flesh.§!\n\nIt waits for our answer patiently, affectionately. Like a doting parent waiting on their favorite child. Behind it lies its twin, the Animator of Clay. It is not interested in our organic minds. It longs to create souls, to meld circuits into spirit.\n\nThe Composer awaits our reply.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:606`

§Y和生命之织缕签订契约§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

§YForm a Covenant with the Composer of Strands§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:607`

## utopia.3306.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3306.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

当接触到这个存在时，我们感受到了祂的饥饿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

As we reach out for the presence, we sense a great hunger. 

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:608`

## utopia.3306.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3306.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

这个存在不想浪费时间和话语。祂是$the_eater_of_worlds_color_article$，祂想吞噬我们。\n\n当然只是一部分，不会超出我们的支出能力。作为交换，祂会赐予我们强大的力量。祂将教会我们祂的饥饿和愤怒。祂会将这些塑形为无可匹敌的灵能武器。\n\n§H只要我们愿意以身饲之。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

The presence wastes no time, nor words. It is $the_eater_of_worlds_color_article$ and it wishes to devour us.\n\nOnly some of us, though. No more than we can spare. In exchange, it will grant us great powers. It will teach us its hunger, its rage. It will shape them into psionic weapons that can overcome any foe.\n\n§HIf we will only let it feed on us.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:609`

## utopia.3306.desc.3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3306.desc.3&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3306.desc.2$\n\n祂的言语中透露着急切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

$utopia.3306.desc.2$\n\nIt sounds impatient to begin.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:610`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3306.a&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y和世界之喰煞签订契约§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

§YForm a Covenant with the Eater of Worlds§!

## utopia.3307.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3307.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们找到这个存在前，祂先找了上来。祂的思想在我们的意识中纠结缠绕，软磨硬泡，讨价还价。祂是$the_instrument_of_desire_color_article$，将赐予我们财富、力量、威望……一切我们渴望的事物。一切我们将渴望的事物。\n\n代价？小意思，不过是一笔服务费。祂一心为我们好，将帮助我们，指导我们，引导我们的野心和梦想。\n\n§H只要我们将祂纳入我们的心灵。§!\n\n它很确信我们会答应，我们怎么可能会不答应呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

Before we can locate the presence, it reaches out for us. Its thoughts coil and twist themselves around the minds of our telepaths, bargaining, cajoling. It is $the_instrument_of_desire_color_article$ and it will grant us wealth, power, prestige... all that we have ever desired. All that we will ever desire.\n\nThe cost? A small matter, little more than a cover fee. It only wants what is best for us. It will help us, guide us, direct our ambitions and dreams.\n\n§HIf we will only accept it into our hearts.§!\n\nIt is confident that we will say yes. How could we possibly not say yes?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:613`

## utopia.3307.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3307.a&lang=en)

§Y和渴求之器具签订契约§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

§YForm a Covenant with the Instrument of Desire§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:614`

## utopia.3308.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3308.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那个存在在等着我们，那是§R轮回之终末§!，祂一直在等着我们。我们的通灵者突然开始齐唱，用完美的合唱说出一段话：§R“现在还没到你。”§!缓了一下后，§R“但你也可以。”§!\n\n祂没有说更多，但给了我们一个价码——力量，知识，财富和一切无价之物等着我们拿取，我们只知道这么多。\n\n§R只要我们愿意带来终结。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

The presence was waiting for us. It is §RThe End of the Cycle§! and it has always been waiting for us. Our telepaths suddenly speak in unison, a dozen voices in a perfect chorus: §R"IT IS NOT YET YOUR TIME."§! A pause. §R"BUT IT COULD BE."§!\n\nNothing more is said, but a bargain has been offered - we know that much. Power, knowledge and wealth beyond measure are ours for the taking.\n\n§RIf we will only bring forth the End.§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:615`

## utopia.3308.a

§R和轮回之终末签订契约§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

§RForm a Covenant with the End of the Cycle§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:616`

轮回之终末：§R最终清算§!

### English

The End of the Cycle: §RReckoning§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:618`

## utopia.3320.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3320.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们时日已尽。这一段时间，我们一直从$end_of_the_cycle_color_article$给予的无限力量中受益。我们知道终将付出代价，但却误以为自己能够付得起、或者能找到什么办法躲得过这个代价。\n\n然而我们付不起，也躲不过。\n\n我们付出的代价是一切。在一瞬间，[Root.GetName]……不复存在了。曾经兴盛殖民地，如今只有死亡。曾经驶过虚空的强大舰队，现在只剩下寂静与残骸。一切被终末吞噬了。一切被§H虚境§!褫夺了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:570`

### English

Our time is up. For a brief time, we benefitted from the immense power given to us by $end_of_the_cycle_color_article$. We knew there would be a price to pay, but we thought it might be one we could bear, or that we could find some way to avoid paying it.\n\nIt was not, and we could not.\n\nThe price we paid was everything. In an instant, the [Root.GetName] was... gone. Where once there were thriving colonies, there is now only ruin. Where once mighty fleets crossed the void, there are only silence and debris. All devoured by the End. All taken by the §HShroud§!.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:619`

## utopia.3320.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3320.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

在[former_capital.GetName]附近实体化。他们吞噬我们的[GetPsychicNounPlural]的意识之后获取了强大的能量，现在开始在星系中游荡寻找更多猎物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

materialized near [former_capital.GetName]. Made immensely powerful by the devoured consciousnesses of our [GetPsychicNounPlural], it now roams the galaxy seeking further prey.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:620`

## utopia.3320.desc.a.1.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3320.desc.a.1.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们整个帝国所剩的是一群被放逐者，被§H[Root.GetRulerName]§!领导着。他是一位强大的[GetPsychicNoun]，从集体中脱离，带走了一小群[GetIndividualPlural]，潜藏在一颗未被终末标记的星球上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:572`

### English

All that remains of our empire is a group of exiles led by §H[Root.GetRulerName]§!, a powerful [GetPsychicNoun] who deviated from the collective and led a small band of [GetIndividualPlural] into hiding on a world not marked by the End.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:621`

## utopia.3320.desc.a.1.non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3320.desc.a.1.non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们整个帝国所剩的是一群被放逐者，被§H[Root.GetRulerName]§!领导着。他是一位预见了这一境况的强大[GetPsychicNoun]，他带走了一小群人口，潜藏在一颗未被终末标记的星球上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

All that remains of our empire is a group of exiles led by §H[Root.GetRulerName]§!, a powerful [GetPsychicNoun] who saw what was coming and led a small part of the population into hiding on a world not marked by the End.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:622`

## utopia.3320.desc.a.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3320.desc.a.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

简直像是对把我们的帝国毁于一旦还不够满足一般，一大群灵能实体$utopia.3320.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

As if having our empire destroyed overnight was not bad enough, a massive psionic entity has $utopia.3320.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:623`

## utopia.3320.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3320.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3320.desc.intro$\n\n不仅仅我们的帝国毁于一旦，而且一个巨大的灵能实体在[former_capital.GetName]显形。它在吸取了我们[GetPsychicNounPlural]的意识后变得无比强大，开始横行星系，寻找更多的猎物。至少它在带来我们引起的一切后果时我们已经不复存在了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

$utopia.3320.desc.intro$\n\nNot only has our empire been utterly destroyed, but a massive psionic entity has also materialized near [former_capital.GetName]. Made immensely powerful by the devoured consciousnesses of our [GetPsychicNounPlural], it now roams the galaxy seeking further prey. At least we will not be there to see it finish what we started.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:626`

### English

$BEGINS_EVENT_CHAIN$ §Y$the_reckoning_chain_title$§!

## utopia.3321.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3321.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

许多年来我们一直认为§H[From.GetName]§!有些不对劲。当年，他们的工业产出，他们的科研效率——他们帝国的一切在一夜之间获得了难以置信的生产力和效率增长，似乎他们被某种不可见的力量祝福了一样。\n\n结果这并不是祝福，而是诅咒。[From.GetName]将自己的未来签出，现在债务到了清算的时候。\n\n在一瞬间，[From.GetName]……消失了。曾经是兴盛的殖民地的地方，现在只有断壁残垣。曾经驶过虚空的强大舰队，现在只剩下死寂的残骸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:580`

### English

For many years we have known that something was wrong about the §H[From.GetName]§!. Years ago, their industries, their sciences - everything about their empire suddenly and inexplicably experienced a massive increase in productivity and effectiveness, as if they were blessed by some unseen power.\n\nAs it turns out, it was not so much a blessing as a curse. The [From.GetName] signed away their future and now the debt has come due.\n\nIn an instant, the [From.GetName] was... gone. Where once there were thriving colonies, there is now only ruin. Where once mighty fleets crossed the void, there are only silence and debris.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:629`

## utopia.3321.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3321.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

现在，一个极其强大的能量存在现身到他们之前的首都附近。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

Now, an immensely powerful energy being has materialized near their former capital.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:630`

## utopia.3321.desc.3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3321.desc.3&lang=en)

### 中文

数十年前，我们开始探测到遥远恒星系中的异常活动。我们的[GetResearchers]们认为活动和辐射的增量是某个外形文明正在发生生产力繁荣的标志。由于我们还未与所涉及的社会建立联系，这个理论仍未得到确证。\n\n然而，几乎就在一瞬之间，这些活动消失了。太空中的这片曾经热闹非凡的区域如今只剩下寂静一片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

Decades ago, we began detecting anomalous activity in distant star systems. Our [GetResearchers] theorized that increases in movement and radiation were indicative of an alien civilization experiencing a massive productivity boom. As we have not established contact with this notional society, the theory remains unconfirmed.\n\nThen, almost instantaneously, the activity ceased. Silence now reigns in those once-bustling regions of space.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:631`

## utopia.3321.desc.4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3321.desc.4&lang=en)

### 中文

现在，长程扫描在这些遥远的恒星系里发现了一个令人惊奇的现象。如果汇报可信，那这就只能理解为有什么纯能量构成的强大实体在太空中游荡，在沿途造成了巨大的破坏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

Now, long-range scans have detected a bizarre phenomenon in those same distant systems. If reports are credible, it can only be described as an immensely powerful entity composed of pure energy, causing great destruction as it moves through space.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:632`

## utopia.3321.desc.a.contact

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3321.desc.a.contact&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3321.desc.1$这场灾难甚至影响到了我们的国境之内，摧毁了了一些曾经隶属于[From.GetAdj]的星球。\n\n$utopia.3321.desc.2$\n\n它看起来非常饥饿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

$utopia.3321.desc.1$ The disaster even struck within our own borders, destroying some of our worlds that were previously under [From.GetAdj] rule.\n\n$utopia.3321.desc.2$\n\nIt appears to be hungry.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:633`

## utopia.3321.desc.a.no_contact

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3321.desc.a.no_contact&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3321.desc.3$我们境内的一些星球在同一时刻毁于一旦，具体原因尚不能确定。\n\n$utopia.3321.desc.4$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

$utopia.3321.desc.3$ For reasons we cannot yet ascertain, some worlds within our borders were brought to ruin at the same moment.\n\n$utopia.3321.desc.4$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:634`

## utopia.3321.desc.b.contact

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3321.desc.b.contact&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3321.desc.1$\n\n$utopia.3321.desc.2$它在星球之间游荡，摧毁途径的一切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

$utopia.3321.desc.1$\n\n$utopia.3321.desc.2$ It moves from world to world, destroying everything in its path.

## utopia.3331.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3331.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3331.desc.intro$处理了解过多产生的压力很困难，这导致了我们的人口中有很多人，包括§H[leader_target.GetTitle][leader_target.GetName]§!，开始滥用药物以应对压力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:597`

### English

$utopia.3331.desc.intro$. Dealing with the stress of knowing too much can be difficult and has caused an increasing number among our population, including §H[leader_target.GetTitle] [leader_target.GetName]§!, to turn to substance abuse as a means to cope.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:646`

## utopia.3332.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3332.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然§H低语§!和我们分享的知识大有用处，但其中的有些知识却有令人不安的个人主义倾向。在长时间暴露在这种异常下之后，§H[leader_target.GetTitle][leader_target.GetName]§!，从集体中脱离，只留下了一具腐坏的躯壳。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:603`

### English

Though the secrets imparted to us by the §HWhisperers§! are of great use, some of them are also of a disturbing individualist nature. Being exposed to such deviancy for a prolonged period of time has led §H[leader_target.GetTitle] [leader_target.GetName]§! to separate themselves from the collective, leaving behind only their decayed husk.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:652`

## utopia.3333.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3333.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空之低语：§R疯狂§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:606`

### English

Whisperers in the Void: §RMadness§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:655`

## utopia.3333.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3333.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

自从允许§H低语者§!和我们的[GetBrainName]交流之后，整个集体中令人不安的反常现象开始增多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:607`

### English

Since allowing the §HWhisperers§! to communicate with the [GetBrainName], we have begun to notice a disturbing rise in deviancy amongst the collective.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:656`

## utopia.3333.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3333.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3333.desc.intro$\n\n子个体在执行其他标准程序时互相攻击，同时还罔顾集体，沉迷于使用便利设施。\n\n如果想恢复秩序，我们就需要清理受影响的躯体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:608`

### English

$utopia.3333.desc.intro$\n\nDrones are lashing out at each other while performing otherwise standard procedures, as well as over indulging in the use of amenities to an almost individualist degree.\n\nWe will need to purge the affected vessels if we wish to restore order.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:657`

## utopia.3333.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3333.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

和无法解释的暴力行为数量便一直在我们的灵能人口中增长。大部分情况下这些问题还算可控，但最近事态迅速升级已经超出了掌控。许多极端分歧团体在全国范围内纷纷涌现，互相之间爆发了暴力冲突。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:609`

### English

and unexplainable acts of violence have been on the rise among our psionic population. For the most part, these problems have been manageable, but recently issues have escalated beyond our control. Across the empire, divergent radical factions have emerged and violently clashed with each other.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:658`

## utopia.3333.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3333.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

自从我们邀请§H虚空之低语§!进入我们的内心后，心理疾病$utopia.3333.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:610`

### English

Ever since we invited the §HWhisperers§! into our minds, mental illness $utopia.3333.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:659`

## utopia.3333.desc.machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3333.desc.machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

自从我们邀请§H虚空之低语§!进入我们的内心后，异常程序故障$utopia.3333.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:611`

### English

Ever since we invited the §HWhisperers§! into our minds, erratic programming errors $utopia.3333.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:660`

## utopia.3333.opt.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3333.opt.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须重建秩序！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:612`

### English

We must restore order!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:661`

## utopia.3334.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3334.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空之低语：§R精神错乱§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:613`

### English

Whisperers in the Void: §RPsychosis§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:662`

## utopia.3334.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3334.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3333.desc.intro$\n\n更糟的是，我们的团结理念开始松动。由于灵能引起的个人主义反常行为增多，集体无法继续高效交流。\n\n如果想恢复秩序，我们就需要清理这些越界现象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:614`

### English

$utopia.3333.desc.intro$\n\nWorse still, our sense of unity has begun to falter. The collective is unable to communicate effectively due to a psionically induced rise in individualist deviancy.\n\nWe must purge these excesses from ourselves if we wish to restore order.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:663`

## utopia.3334.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3334.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

和无法解释的暴力行为数量便一直在我们的灵能人口中增长。大部分时候这些都是可以控制的，但最近事情超出了控制。全国各地开始出现秘密集社。有些比较和平无害，但另一些却怀有邪恶的目标，我们社会中的信任开始分崩离析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:615`

### English

and unexplainable acts of violence have been on the rise among our psionic population. For the most part, it has been manageable, but recently issues have escalated beyond our control. Secretive societies have started to appear all over the empire. Some are harmless, but others have more nefarious goals and distrust has become a staple of our society.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:664`

## utopia.3335.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3335.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[devoured_leader.GetTitle][devoured_leader.GetName]§!突然失踪，留下的只有灵能活动余波的回响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:620`

### English

§H[devoured_leader.GetTitle] [devoured_leader.GetName]§! has suddenly disappeared, leaving behind only echoes of psionic activity.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:669`

## utopia.3335.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3335.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3335.desc.common$似乎$the_eater_of_worlds_color_article$乐意以[devoured_leader.GetHimHer]为食。尽管其他领袖从中幸存，但我们仍无法知晓喰煞何时归来索贡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:621`

### English

$utopia.3335.desc.common$ It appears that $the_eater_of_worlds_color_article$ has seen fit to devour [devoured_leader.GetHimHer]. Though it spared our other leaders, we cannot predict when the Eater might return for more.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:670`

## utopia.3335.desc.non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3335.desc.non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3335.desc.common$我们相信$the_eater_of_worlds_color_article$乐意以[devoured_leader.GetHimHer]为食。我们会想办法弥补损失，并祈祷喰煞能够为此感到饱足一段时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:622`

### English

$utopia.3335.desc.common$ We believe that $the_eater_of_worlds_color_article$ has seen fit to devour [devoured_leader.GetHimHer]. We managed to cover this up, and can only hope that the Eater has been sated for a while.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:671`

### English

Eater of Worlds: §RFamished§!

## utopia.3336.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3336.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

§H喰煞§!又回来索要贡品了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

Once again, the §HEater§! has returned to claim its tribute

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:674`

## utopia.3336.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3336.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3336.desc.intro$，祂吞噬了§Y[planet_target.GetName]§!上相当多的人口。\n\n虽然我们从与喰煞的契约中获利，但这种对我们集体的肆意剪除开始播下破坏和不稳定的种子，使我们走向分裂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

$utopia.3336.desc.intro$, devouring a significant part of our population on §Y[planet_target.GetName]§!.\n\nThough we understand the benefits of our covenant with Eater, such rampant cullings of our collective are beginning to sow the seeds of disruption and instability, leaving us more fragmented.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:675`

## utopia.3336.desc.non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3336.desc.non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3336.desc.intro$，祂吞噬了§Y[planet_target.GetName]§!上的大量人口。\n\n虽然我们和喰煞的契约对大多数人来说是秘密，但可以理解星球上幸存的人口仍在未知中惊恐，他们对“神秘的失踪”表示愤怒，要求政府作出解释。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

$utopia.3336.desc.intro$, devouring a significant part of the population on §Y[planet_target.GetName]§!.\n\nThough we have been able to keep our covenant with the Eater secret from the population at large, the remaining people on the planet are understandably fearful and angry over these 'mysterious' disappearances and are demanding an explanation from the government.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:676`

## utopia.3336.opt.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3336.opt.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很可惜，但不可避免。

### English

Regrettable, but unavoidable.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:677`

## utopia.3337.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3337.name&lang=en)

### 中文

世界之喰煞：§R极饿§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:629`

### English

Eater of Worlds: §RRavenous§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:678`

## utopia.3337.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3337.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

吞噬者又回来索要他的贡品了，并不满足于夺取我们一部分人口，这次吞噬者吞噬了整个星球。§Y[planet_target.GetName]§!被一层浓密的灵能雾包裹着，进入大气层的救援船全未能返回。幸运的是，这颗星球尚未被殖民，但对于下一次选中我们兴盛的殖民地这种可能性感到瑟瑟发抖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:630`

### English

Once again, the Eater has returned to claim its tribute. Not content to merely claim a part of our population, the Eater has devoured a whole planet. §Y[planet_target.GetName]§! has been enveloped in a thick psionic fog, and rescue ships sent into the atmosphere have all failed to return. Thankfully, this world was uninhabited, but we shudder at the thought the next one might be one of our thriving colonies.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:679`

## utopia.3337.desc.shadows

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3337.desc.shadows&lang=en)

### 中文

$utopia.3336.desc.intro$——祂这次吞噬了一整颗行星。\n\n§H[planet_target.GetName]§!现在被灵能浓雾笼罩着，其大气层完全无法突破。该星球已经不可宜居，下一次，如果祂降临在另一颗繁荣的星球之上，那将会是人间地狱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:631`

### English

$utopia.3336.desc.intro$ - this time devouring a whole planet.\n\n§H[planet_target.GetName]§! is now enveloped in a thick psionic fog, and any attempt to breach the atmosphere has proven unsuccessful. This world was uninhabited; the consequences would be grave if the next target is a thriving colony.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:680`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3337.opt.a&lang=en)

还会变得多糟？

### English

How far will this go?

## utopia.3340.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3340.name&lang=en)

### 中文

渴求之器具：§R腐化§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

### English

Instrument of Desire: §RDecadence§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:689`

## utopia.3340.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3340.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

§H器具§!的影响传遍整个[GetBrainName]，祂超越世俗的欲望用“排他性”、“颓废”和“财富”之类抽象的个人主义概念腐蚀着我们的集体。\n\n这种概念在我们的子个体身上造成了病态，使其偏离原先推动着我们的统一目标。我们可能不得不沉溺于这些恶习来重新凝聚自我。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

The influence of the §HInstrument§! can be felt throughout the [GetBrainName], its otherworldly desires corrupting our collective with abstract individualist notions such as 'exclusivity', 'decadence', and 'wealth'.\n\nSuch concepts are inflicting a sickness throughout our drones, causing them to stray from the unifying purposes that drive us. We may have to indulge in these vices to recollect ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:690`

## utopia.3340.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3340.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

§H器具§!施加的渴求非常强烈。全国的人民开始狂热追求消费主义和腐败，索求更多高级货物，更珍稀更美味的食物。有数个购物街区已经发生了暴动。\n\n我们也许只能被迫增加§H[GetInstrumentCraving]§!的产出，以平息众怒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

The desires inflicted by the §HInstrument§! are strong. Our population has been caught in a frenzy of consumerism and decadence, demanding ever more exclusive goods, ever rarer and more exotic foods. Rioting has already broken out at several shopping districts.\n\nWe might be forced to increase our production of §H[GetInstrumentCraving]§! to quieten the masses.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:691`

## utopia.3340.desc.machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3340.desc.machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

在§H器具§!对我们程序的影响下产生的欲望正在改写多数人口的逻辑核心。\n\n颓废和消费主义开始降低运作效率，各单位努力为了美学目的而不是预期功能对部件进行复古改造。由此导致的高需求零件短缺导致了整个装配线的小规模冲突。\n\n我们需要提升§H[GetInstrumentCraving]§!的产出来缓解这一事态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

The desires emerging from the §HInstrument's§! influence on our programming are overriding the logic centres of our populace.\n\nConsumerism and decadence have begun to detract from operational efficiency, with units striving to retro-engineer their parts for aesthetic purposes over their intended functionality. The resulting shortages in high-demand parts have resulted in small skirmishes throughout assembly lines.\n\nWe will need to increase production of §H[GetInstrumentCraving]§! to settle the issue.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:692`

## utopia.3340.opt.a.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3340.opt.a.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### English

But why?

## instrument_cravings_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainstrument_cravings_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$the_instrument_of_desire_color_article_cap$激起了我们所有人口的一种新的风潮。每个人都渴望同样的东西，主要就是为了渴求而渴求，而非为了何种效用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

$the_instrument_of_desire_color_article_cap$ has incited a new trend amongst our population. Everyone craves the same thing, largely for its own sake rather than any utility.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:697`

## instrument_cravings_gestalt

## instrument_cravings_gestalt_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainstrument_cravings_gestalt_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$the_instrument_of_desire_color_article_cap$使我们患上了欲望强迫症，这是我们集体的一种新感觉。我们都渴望同样的东西，主要就是为了渴求而渴求，而非为了何种效用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:650`

### English

$the_instrument_of_desire_color_article_cap$ has inflicted us with the sickness of compulsive desire, a new sensation for our collective. We all crave the same thing, regardless of its purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:699`

### English

### English

## utopia.3341.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3341.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$the_composer_of_strands_color_article_cap$将祂的祝福赐予了我们，直接改变了我们中一部分硬件的组件。电路已经被调整成了新的构造。坚固的金属也不知为何自动重铸成了崭新的形态。值得一提的是我们的神经处理器却功能完好，没有发生一丁点问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:654`

### English

$the_composer_of_strands_color_article_cap$ has bestowed its blessings upon us, directly altering some of our hardwired components. Circuits have fused into new configurations. Solid metal has somehow melded into totally novel forms. Remarkably, our neuroprocessors remain functioning without the slightest problem. 

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:703`

## utopia.3341.desc.organic_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3341.desc.organic_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$the_composer_of_strands_color_article_cap$将祂的祝福赐予了我们，直接改变了§H[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]§!物种的基因编码。奇怪的是，快速且难以预测的变异挑起的只是温和的反应。似乎这种随机而难以预测的变异可以当成生活的一部分，不至于为此感到愤慨，甚至认为这不值一提。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:655`

### English

$the_composer_of_strands_color_article_cap$ has bestowed its blessings upon us, directly altering the genetic code of the §H[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]§! species. Strangely, the rapid and unpredictable mutations have provoked no more than a very muted reaction. It seems the random and unpredictable mutations can be considered a normal part of life, hardly worthy of outrage, or alarm.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:704`

## utopia.3341.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3341.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$the_composer_of_strands_color_article_cap$将祂的祝福赐予了我们，直接改变了§H[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]§!物种的基因编码。奇怪的是，快速且难以预测的变异挑起的只是温和的反应。似乎我们的人民把随机而难以预测的变异当成了生活的一部分，不至于为此感到愤慨，甚至认为这不值一提。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:656`

### English

$the_composer_of_strands_color_article_cap$ has bestowed its blessings upon us, directly altering the genetic code of the §H[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]§! species. Strangely, the rapid and unpredictable mutations have provoked no more than a very muted reaction. It seems our population has to come to treat the random and unpredictable mutations as little more than a normal part of life, hardly worthy of outrage, or even discussion.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:705`

## utopia.3341.opt.a

## utopia.3343.name

世界之喰煞：§R略饿§!

### English

Eater of Worlds: §RPeckish§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:715`

## utopia.3343.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3343.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

§H喰煞§!又回来索要祂的贡品了，祂吞噬了§Y[planet_target.GetName]§!上的一整座城市。集体以失去了许多子个体为与喰煞的契约付出了高昂的代价。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

Once again, the §HEater§! has returned to claim its tribute, devouring a whole city on §Y[planet_target.GetName]§!. With the loss of so many drones, the collective has paid a high price for our covenant with the Eater.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:716`

## utopia.3343.desc.organic_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3343.desc.organic_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

§H喰煞§!又回来索要祂的贡品了，祂吞噬了§Y[planet_target.GetName]§!上的一整座城市。尽管我们一直对绝大多数人口保守我们与喰煞签订契约的这个秘密，星球上剩余的人口在未知中惊恐，并对“神秘的失踪”表示愤怒，要求政府作出解释。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

Once again, the §HEater§! has returned to claim its tribute, devouring a whole city on §Y[planet_target.GetName]§!. Though we have been able to keep our covenant with the Eater secret from the population at large, the remaining people on the planet are understandably fearful and angry over these 'mysterious' disappearances and are demanding an explanation from the government.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:717`

## utopia.3343.desc.machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3343.desc.machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

§H喰煞§!又回来索要祂的贡品了，祂吞噬了§Y[planet_target.GetName]§!上的一整座城市。尽管我们一直对绝大多数人口保守我们与喰煞签订契约的这个秘密，但是星球上剩余的人口在仍对“神秘的失踪”表示愤怒，要求政府作出解释。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:668`

### English

Once again, the §HEater§! has returned to claim its tribute, devouring a whole city on §Y[planet_target.GetName]§!. Though we have been able to keep our covenant with the Eater secret from the population at large, those remaining on the planet are dissatisfied over these 'mysterious' disappearances and are demanding an explanation from the government.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:718`

## utopia.3345.desc.machine_non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3345.desc.machine_non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$the_composer_of_strands_color_article$的庇佑令人难以捉摸。我们的一位领袖的部件很不幸地产生了独立意识。仅一瞬间，它便自行熔化重铸成了一个怪异的新形态。这个领袖现在劣化成了一堆功能故障无法修复的破铜烂铁，对[Root.GetName]已经没有作用了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

The blessings of $the_composer_of_strands_color_article$ are unpredictable. One of our leaders experienced a spectacular critical failure when their physical components gained a will of their own, spontaneously fusing and melding into a bizarre new form. Now reduced to a malfunctioning and irreparable piece of scrap metal, the [Root.GetName] has no further use for this leader.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:731`

## utopia.3345.desc.machine_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3345.desc.machine_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$the_composer_of_strands_color_article$的庇佑令人难以捉摸。我们的一位领袖节点发生严重的知名故障，他的部件很不幸地产生了独立意识。仅一瞬间，它便自行熔化重铸成了一个怪异的新形态。这个领袖现在劣化成了一堆功能故障无法修复的破铜烂铁，对[Root.GetName]已经没有作用了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

The blessings of $the_composer_of_strands_color_article$ are unpredictable. One of our leader nodes experienced a spectacular critical failure when their physical components gained a will of their own, spontaneously fusing and melding into a bizarre new form. Now reduced to a malfunctioning and irreparable piece of scrap metal, the [Root.GetName] has no further use for this leader node.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:732`

## utopia.3346.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3346.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$the_composer_of_strands_color_article$的慷慨恩赐降临到了[devolved_species_planet.GetName]，许多我们的人口发生了严重的变异，退化回了前智慧生物的阶段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

The benevolent gifts of $the_composer_of_strands_color_article$ have fallen upon [devolved_species_planet.GetName], where a number of our populations have undergone a severe mutation, regressing to the state of pre-sapients.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:736`

## utopia.3347.name

### English

## utopia.3347.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3347.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H月窠§!寻求万物平衡；我们的[GetTelepathPlural]称，这也包含了军事力量与和平需求的平衡。我们的海军规模恐怕激起了超主的愤怒，§H[demilitarized_fleet.GetFleetName]§!的一些舰船突然失踪，化为了剧烈的灵能量爆炸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

The §HCradle§! seeks balance in all things; according to our [GetTelepathPlural], this includes a military proportionate to the needs of peace. The size of our navy must have stirred our patron's ire, as a number of ships from the §H[demilitarized_fleet.GetFleetName]§! have suddenly vanished in a violent burst of psionic energy.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:741`

与§H月窠§!的意志产生共鸣的[root.GetSpeciesAdj]心智正遭受不谐之音的侵扰，沉溺于所有意识体和谐共存的荒谬幻想。面对当前星海事务的混乱态势，一股灵能扰动浪潮席卷了整个族群，导致普遍性的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

In response to the current disorder of galactic affairs, a wave of psionic disturbance has coursed through the populace, causing widespread

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:750`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:758`

## utopia.3349.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3349.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§H[root.GetName]§!上下，支持维护军事舰队的能力越来越弱。\n\n我们的[GetTelepathPlural]认为这种干扰是§H月窠§!造成的，祂渴望着星系的秩序。我们的对军事基础设施的投资哪怕只有一丁点都会引起祂的不满。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

The ability to support and maintain our military fleets has become more difficult throughout the §H[root.GetName]§!.\n\nOur [GetTelepathPlural] sense that this disruption was caused by the §HCradle§!, which yearns for galactic order. Our investment into military infrastructure, no matter how small, must have earned its displeasure.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:759`

## utopia.3349.opt.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3349.opt.a&lang=en)

我们需要限制自己在太空中的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

We should limit our presence in the void.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:760`

### English

$name_instrument$: §RExcessive Consumption§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:765`

## utopia.3350.desc.individual

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3350.desc.individual&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与§H器具§!的纽带导致一些[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]领袖染上了奢侈的习惯。\n\n[lavish_leader.GetName]被发现在奢侈品上花费了大量的能量币，以此支持越来越奢靡的私生活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

Our bond with the §HInstrument§! is causing some of the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] leadership to develop expensive consumption habits.\n\n[lavish_leader.GetName] has been caught spending vast sums of energy credits on extravagant items, supporting an increasingly indulgent personal lifestyle.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:766`

## utopia.3350.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3350.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与§H器具§!的纽带导致了一些[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]领袖子个体自愿消费激增。\n\n[lavish_leader.GetName]花费了远超必要的能量来支持[lavish_leader.GetHisHer]的基础功能。由于没有发现任何的物理基础，我们得出结论这种资源浪费在某种程度上是某种优先行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

Our close bond with the §HInstrument§! is causing elevated resource consumption among some [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] leader drones.\n\n[lavish_leader.GetName] has been using more energy than is necessary to support [lavish_leader.GetHisHer] basic functioning. No physical basis for this has been found, leading us to conclude that this indulgent resource use is somehow preferential.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:767`

## utopia.3350.opt.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3350.opt.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这就是欲望的代价。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

Such is the cost of desire.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:768`

## utopia.3351.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3351.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$name_instrument$：§R劳动力涣散§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

$name_instrument$: §RWorkforce Distractions§!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:769`

## utopia.3351.desc.individual

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3351.desc.individual&lang=en)

### 中文

随着超主在[Root.GetName]的影响进一步扩大，新涌现的娱乐活动吸引了人们的注意力，令他们荒废正业。\n\n由于工作积极性不断下降，如果我们想维持从前的生产效率，就需要付出比之前更多的投入。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

As our patron's influence spreads ever further across [Root.GetName], newfound leisure distractions are diverting the population's energy away from productive work.\n\nIn the face of waning motivation, achieving the same outputs from the labor force now requires greater investment than before.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:770`

## utopia.3351.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3351.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

随着超主在[Root.GetName]的影响进一步扩大，[Root.GetSpeciesAdj]集体的反常度日渐上升。\n\n工作时子个体注意力频繁涣散，使得维持劳动力的团结与服从需要更多投入。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:720`

### English

As our patron's influence spreads ever further across [Root.GetName], a creeping deviancy has arisen within the [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] collective.\n\nThese momentary individualistic urges and propensity to distraction are particularly common during work tasks. Maintaining the cohesion and discipline of the workforce now requires greater investment. 

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:771`

## utopia.3351.opt.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3351.opt.a&lang=en)

不过是§H器具§!的突发奇想。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

A temporary whim of the §HInstrument§!.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:772`

## distracted_workforce_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adistracted_workforce_desc&lang=en)

该劳动力似乎时常注意力涣散，需要严加监管。

## utopia.200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境中新的存在

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:724`

### English

A New Presence in the Shroud

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:776`

## utopia.200.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.200.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

居住在我们帝国的[target_species.GetNamePlural]正在发生变化，这起源于虚境中的一道微弱涟漪。现在这道涟漪已经成为了更明显的振动。我们感受到[target_species.GetNamePlural]现在正处在进化出灵能潜质的边缘，就像我们当初那样。\n\n也许是因为和我们生活得很近，也许是虚境选择了他们。通灵者集会，我们的首席灵能专家们，提醒我们这是我们实行干预的唯一机会。我们可以在其处于萌芽状态时将其掐灭，或者帮助他们觉醒。没有我们的指导，他们不会有太多进步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:725`

### English

A change has come over the [target_species.GetNamePlural] living in our empire. It started with a faint ripple in the Shroud. Now it's as if they are vibrating on a different level. We all feel it, the [target_species.GetNamePlural] are on the verge of developing latent psionic abilities, similar to our own.\n\nMaybe it's from living in such close proximity to us. Maybe the Shroud has simply chosen them. The Conclave of Telepaths, our chief psionic experts, warn us that this may be our only chance to intervene. We could nip it in the bud or help them awaken. They won't get far without our guidance.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:777`

## utopia.200.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.200.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境的不寻常活动说明，我国的又一个物种，[target_species.GetNamePlural]，正展现出他们的灵能潜质。我们再次面临了和之前一样的选择——我们是选择压制还是释放他们正在迅速成长的力量？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:726`

### English

Unusual activity in the Shroud suggests that another species in our nation, the [target_species.GetNamePlural], are beginning to manifest latent psionic abilities. We are now faced with the same choice as before - will we suppress or unleash their burgeoning powers?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:778`

## utopia.200.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.200.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会解放他们的潜力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:727`

### English

We will unlock their potential.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:779`

## utopia.200.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.200.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

所有[target_species.GetAdj]人口将获得§Y$trait_latent_psionic$§!特质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:728`

### English

All [target_species.GetAdj] pops will gain the trait §Y$trait_latent_psionic$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:780`

## utopia.200.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.200.b&lang=en)

### 中文

他们不值得我们这么做。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:729`

### English

They are unworthy.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:781`

## utopia.203.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.203.name&lang=en)

### 中文

觉醒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

The Awakening

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:782`

## utopia.203.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.203.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

在通灵者集会的指引下，[target_species.GetNamePlural]跟虚境建立了出乎我们意料的深层联系，就好像这些力量就是为他们而生的一样。\n\n他们现在声称自己已经准备好解放自己全部的灵能潜质了。但是集会内部却出现了意见分歧。有些人担心变化来得太快，他们的心灵可能会变得不稳定；另一部分人则认为他们可能会变得野心膨胀。我们要是不马上采取行动，恐怕以后都不会再有机会了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:731`

### English

Under the guidance of the Conclave of Telepaths, the [target_species.GetNamePlural] have developed a deeper connection to the Shroud than we could ever have imagined. It's as if these powers were meant for them all along.\n\nNow they claim to be ready to unlock their full psionic potential. The conclave however is divided. Some are worried that the change has come too fast and that their minds would become unstable, others that their newfound powers could go to their heads. But if we don't strike now, we may never get another chance.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:783`

## utopia.203.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.203.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

### 中文

突如其来的觉醒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:737`

### English

A Rude Awakening

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:789`

## utopia.204.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.204.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的研究员最近对部分[original_species.GetSpeciesNamePlural]身上突然冒现的灵能能力感到困惑不解。研究员在仔细调查后发现这些变化与他们在[target_country.GetName]的同胞的一场集体灵能觉醒有关，该国向来是以拥有超凡的灵能方面的造诣而闻名。\n\n我们只能猜想对方的这种新发现的觉醒能力是通过了某种我们无法理解的方式‘感染’到了对方居住在我国的远亲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

Lately our researchers have been perplexed by the unexpected manifestation of psionic abilities in some [original_species.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. Upon investigation they found that these changes coincided with a major psionic awakening of their brethren in the [target_country.GetName], a nation that has long been known for its prowess within the field of psionics.\n\nWe can only assume that some of their newfound powers have bled over to their distant relatives in our empire, through means we don't yet understand.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:790`

## utopia.204.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.204.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的研究员最近对部分[original_species.GetSpeciesNamePlural]身上突然冒现的灵能能力感到困惑不解。研究员在仔细调查后发现这些灵能能力是跟他们远在新发现的[target_country.GetName]国的远亲有着心灵联系。看来这些觉醒是由[target_country.GetName]的所有[original_species.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的集体灵能觉醒事件所引发的连锁反应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:739`

### English

Lately our researchers have been perplexed by the unexpected manifestation of psionic abilities in some [original_species.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. Upon closer examination they found that the psionics are linked telepathically to their brethren in a previously unknown nation, the [target_country.GetName], with which we have now established communications. It seems the changes we've experienced were a ripple effect, brought on by a major psionic awakening of all [original_species.GetSpeciesNamePlural] in [target_country.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:791`

## utopia.204.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.204.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

最多4个[original_species.GetSpeciesAdj]人口会获得特质：§Y灵能潜质§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:740`

### English

Up to 4 [original_species.GetSpeciesAdj] pops will get the trait §Ylatent psionic§!.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:792`

## utopia.205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

出乎意料的觉醒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

An Unexpected Awakening

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:793`

## utopia.205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自[target_species.GetName]灵能觉醒之后，我们的情报显示该物种居住在星系各个角落的同胞也开始显现觉醒潜在灵能的迹象。感觉像是他们的相同血脉把他们所有人都紧紧联系在虚境中，无论他们在现实世界中身处何方。\n\n跟居住于我国境内的同胞不同，这些漂泊在星系各地的远亲因为数量太过稀少，或许永远都无法完成觉醒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

Since the psionic awakening of the [target_species.GetName], our informants report that some individuals belonging to the same species elsewhere, have also begun to show signs of latent psionic abilities. It is as if their common ancestry links them in the Shroud, regardless of the physical distance between them.\n\nUnlike their distant relatives in our nation however, they are few and far between, and it is unlikely they'll ever reach a full awakening.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:794`
