## instrument_deviance_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainstrument_deviance_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球上的人口正背离帝国的道德并追求着他们各自的目的。毫无疑问这是§Y渴求之器具§!的所作所为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:764`

### English

The population of this planet is turning away from our empire's ethics to pursue their own goals. This is undoubtedly the doing of the §YInstrument of Desire§!.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:819`

## d_eater_deposit_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_eater_deposit_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星的一部分被一个§H虚境§!实体吞噬。造成的深坑萦绕着盘蜷的紫色雾气，附近区域的人很容易发生失踪。胆敢接近者都一去不复返了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

Part of this planet has been devoured by a §HShroud§! entity. The resulting crater is home to swirling purple mists, and those that venture nearby often never return.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:826`

## covenant.whisperers.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acovenant.whisperers.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

完成了，我们和$whisperers_in_the_void_color_article$签订了契约，并且他们已开始和我们的科学家和领袖们分享秘密。我们从他们的知识中受益匪浅。\n\n唯一的坏处就是人口总体的精神疾病和自杀率瞬间上升，并且我们的有些星球上正经受一系列无法解释的谋杀和袭击，而被指控人却情绪稳定并很适应社会。我们的诊所和精神病院对患者的大量入院措手不及。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:779`

### English

It is done. We have formed a Covenant with $whisperers_in_the_void_color_article$ and already they have begun to share their secrets with our scientists and leaders. We will benefit immensely from their knowledge.\n\nThe only downside appears to be a sudden upswing in mental illness and suicide among the general population, and some of our worlds are experiencing a flurry of unexplainable murders and assaults committed by otherwise stable and well-adjusted individuals. Our clinics and asylums are having some trouble dealing with the sudden influx of patients.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:837`

## covenant.whisperers.option

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acovenant.whisperers.option&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能处理好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

We can handle it.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:838`

## covenant.eater.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acovenant.eater.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

完成了，我们和$the_eater_of_worlds_color_article$订了契约，我们的陆军和海军已经能感受到影响了。士兵们战斗时的残暴仅次于野蛮人，甚至我们舰船的每件武器都在渴求着敌人的死亡。\n\n我们有个殖民地中的一整城市的人口瞬间神秘失踪，这毫无疑问与之有关，毕竟这是我们答应给喰煞的祭品。\n\n看起来付出的代价微不足道，当然，前提是喰煞的欲壑已经被填平了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:781`

### English

It is done. We have formed a Covenant with $the_eater_of_worlds_color_article$ and already the effects can be felt in our army and navy. Our soldiers now fight with a ferocity that borders on savagery and even the very weapons of our ship somehow seem to hunger for the deaths of our enemies.\n\nUndoubtedly related is the sudden and mysterious disappearance of the entire population of a city on one of our colonies, its people taken by the Eater as its promised tribute.\n\nIt seems a small price to pay - assuming, of course, that the Eater will not get hungry again...

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:839`

## covenant.eater.option

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acovenant.eater.option&lang=en)

### 中文

但愿他不饿了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:782`

### English

Let's hope not.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:840`

## covenant.instrument.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acovenant.instrument.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

完成了，我们和$the_instrument_of_desire_color_article$签订了契约。没过多久我们就感受到了效果：我们收到报告称在矿井中找到了新的富矿脉，破产的企业几乎在一夜之间重新蓬勃发展，珍稀资源在我们之前认为只有冰和岩石的小行星上找到。\n\n看起来我们的帝国如同器具所保佑的一般进入了一个财富和繁荣的黄金时代。但，祂说的代价是什么……？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:783`

### English

It is done. We have formed a Covenant with $the_instrument_of_desire_color_article$. It did not take long for the effects to be felt: reports of rich new veins of ore found in our mines; businesses that were about to fail suddenly booming; rare and valuable resources found on asteroids that we were absolutely certain previously held nothing but ice and rock.\n\nIt seems our empire is entering a golden age of wealth and prosperity, just as the Instrument promised us - but what was the cost it spoke of...?

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:841`

## covenant.instrument.option

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acovenant.instrument.option&lang=en)

### 中文

我确定不是什么很不好的事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:784`

### English

I'm sure it's nothing bad.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:842`

## covenant.composer.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acovenant.composer.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

完成了，我们和$the_composer_of_strands_color_article$签订了契约。不久我们就感受到了效果：瞬间且明显的生育率上升显然是织缕的所为。然而，有伴随着这些正面效果，我们也在经历大变异的爆发：出生的后代有额外的肢体和器官……些人生出和他们的解剖学形态完全无相似的孩子。\n\n幸运的是他们造成的几例死亡完全被高生育率和人民健康状况提高给盖过了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:785`

### English

It is done. We have formed a Covenant with $the_composer_of_strands_color_article$. It did not take long for the effects to be felt: a sudden and significant increase in birth rates that is undoubtedly the work of the Composer. However, along with these positive effects we are also experiencing a rash of strange new mutations: offspring born with extra limbs and organs... some of which bear no resemblance to any previously cataloged part of their anatomy.\n\nFortunately, the few deaths resulting from them are more than outweighed by the higher birth rates and the apparent increased vitality of our people.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:843`

## covenant.composer.option

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acovenant.composer.option&lang=en)

### 中文

我们做了正确的事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:786`

### English

We've done the right thing.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:844`

## covenant.cycle.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acovenant.cycle.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

完成了，我们和§R轮回之终末§!签订了契约。当我们和他定下契约时，他说了也只说了三个字§R五十年§!。然后，只有沉默，我们思索着我们为自己的未来带来了什么样的后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:787`

### English

It is done. We have formed a Covenant with the §REnd of the Cycle§!. As we took its bargain, a mere two words were spoken: §R"FIFTY YEARS"§!. Then, silence, as we were left to contemplate the future consequences of what we have just brought onto ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:845`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acovenant.cycle.option&lang=en)

### English

May the spirits have mercy on us all.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:846`

### 中文

虚空之低语

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:827`

### English

The Whisperers in the Void

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:887`

## whisperers_covenant_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awhisperers_covenant_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与一位名为$whisperers_in_the_void_color_article$的虚境实体建立了纽带。伴随着莫测的感觉，它索取一定代价赐予我们零星力量。这样的交换应该还会有更多。\n\n我们的[GetTelepathPlural]总结道，如果将我们的行为与超主保持一致，这条纽带将更容易被加强。\n\n低语享受新的知识、沉默的秘密和阴谋。祂们陶醉于一种简单的快乐，这种快乐充满了头脑，在接缝处开裂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:828`

### English

We have created a bond with a Shroud entity named $whisperers_in_the_void_color_article$. Unfathomable, it granted us crumbs of its power for a price. There will likely be more to follow.\n\nOur [GetTelepathPlural] theorize that the bond can be deepened more easily if we align our actions with the will of our patron.\n\nThe Whisperers enjoy new knowledge, silent secrets, and conspiracies. They revel in the simple joy of a mind filled to the brim, cracking at the seams.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:888`

## eater_covenant_chain_desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aeater_covenant_chain_desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与一个名为$the_eater_of_worlds_color_article$的虚境实体建立了纽带。祂索取一定代价赐予我们零星力量。这样的交换应该还会有更多。\n\n我们的[GetTelepathPlural]推论，只看我们的行为与超主的意志相调和，这条纽带就会进一步加强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:832`

### English

We have created a bond with a Shroud entity named $the_eater_of_worlds_color_article$. Insatiable, it granted us crumbs of its power for a price. There will likely be more to follow.\n\nOur [GetTelepathPlural] theorize that the bond can be deepened more easily if we align our actions with the will of our patron.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:892`

## composer_covenant_chain_desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acomposer_covenant_chain_desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与一位名为$the_composer_of_strands_color_article$的虚境实体建立了纽带。伴随着荫蔽的感觉，祂索取一定代价赐予我们零星力量。这样的交换应该还会有更多。\n\n我们的[GetTelepathPlural]推论，只看我们的行为与超主的意志相调和，这条纽带就会进一步加强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:837`

### English

We have created a bond with a Shroud entity named $the_composer_of_strands_color_article$. Sheltering, it granted us crumbs of its power for a price. There will likely be more to follow.\n\nOur [GetTelepathPlural] theorize that the bond can be further deepened more easily if we align our actions with the will of our patron.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:897`

## composer_covenant_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acomposer_covenant_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$composer_covenant_chain_desc.intro$\n\n织缕渴求基因操控和将血肉从进化的桎梏中解脱。祂教导我们对敬畏生命和自然，以及血肉的神圣性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:838`

### English

$composer_covenant_chain_desc.intro$\n\nThe Composer yearns for genetic manipulations and flesh set free from the constraints of evolution. It tells us about respect for life and nature, and the sanctity of the flesh.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:898`

## utopia.2671.name

### 中文

我们的纽带加深了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:846`

### English

Our Bond Grows Deeper

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:907`

## utopia.2671.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2671.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对自己超主的理解与日俱增。我们对于祂来说只不过是茶余饭后的闲思遐绪。房室内的虫子。然而，是祂对我们降下了恩宠，而且非常偶然地，它对我们施予了又一汪能源之滴露。\n\n我们的灵能使现在和超主愈发地协调，受到鼓舞我们计划建立一座纪念碑来铭记我们的刻印，在这里我们最为杰出的灵能使可以尝试与§Y[GetShroudPatron]§!进行沟通。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

Our understanding of our patron grows. We are no more than an afterthought to it. Insects in its home. Yet, it has taken a liking to us and on this occasion, it has bestowed another drop of power upon us.\n\nOur telepaths are now more closely attuned to our patron, and we have been inspired to create a monument to celebrate our covenant, a place where our best telepaths can try to commune with the §Y[GetShroudPatron]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:908`

## utopia.2676.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2676.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H虚境§!的神秘对于我们来说异乎寻常，难以消弭，但是我们和§Y[GetShroudPatron]§!的协调更加融顺了。无论我们去往哪里，我们都能感受到它在我们思绪的背后，它的能量渗过心灵的缝隙进入我们的现实。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

The mysteries of the §HShroud§! are too alien for us to dispel, but we have become more attuned to the §Y[GetShroudPatron]§!. Everywhere we go, we can sense it at the back of our minds, and its power seeps into our reality from the cracks of our psyche.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:919`

### English

### English

Scraps of Power

## utopia.2681.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2681.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的超主，§H[GetShroudPatron]§!，从§H虚境§!中沉积出了一小份能量以备取用。与超主的紧密关系让我们能安全吸收这些能量。\n\n我们挑选了一些候选人，但只有其中一位可以得到获得汲取超主力量的殊荣。我们必须做出抉择。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:863`

### English

Our patron, the §H[GetShroudPatron]§!, has deposited a fraction of its energy in a fold of the §HShroud§!, ready for the taking. Our close bond will allow one of us to safely absorb this power.\n\nA number of candidates have been selected, but only one of them can be granted the privilege to wield the power of our patron. We must decide who it shall be.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:926`

## utopia.2682.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2682.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

完成了。随着力量被注入到[chosen_leader.GetName]体内，身旁的人们都能[GetFeelLoc]到这灵能显昭的分量，这让他们肃然起敬。[chosen_leader.GetHeSheCap]现在成为了[GetImmortalLoc]的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:869`

### English

It is done. As the power was invested in [chosen_leader.GetName], all those around could [GetFeelLoc] the weight of its psionic presence. It is powerful and commands respect. [chosen_leader.GetHeSheCap] will now be the [GetImmortalLoc] 

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:933`

### English

$utopia.2682.desc.intro$ champion of the §H[GetShroudPatron]§!.

### English

## utopia.2682.desc.previous_chosen_killed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2682.desc.previous_chosen_killed&lang=en)

### 中文

这次事件给§H[previous_chosen_leader.GetName]§!，一位曾被旧主选中的异端，带来了毁灭性打击。在[previous_chosen_leader.GetHisHer]的流放地，随着最后一丝灵能从[previous_chosen_leader.GetHisHer]支离破碎的躯体中消散，这位异端的生命也随之走到了尽头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:872`

### English

The event had a devastating effect on the heretic §H[previous_chosen_leader.GetName]§!, once considered the Chosen of our former patron. From [previous_chosen_leader.GetHisHer] place of exile, [previous_chosen_leader.GetHeShe] quietly expired when the last traces of psionic power ebbed from [previous_chosen_leader.GetHisHer] broken form.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:936`

## utopia.2682.desc.previous_chosen_lost_patron

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2682.desc.previous_chosen_lost_patron&lang=en)

### 中文

该事件对§H[chosen_leader_lost_patron.GetName]§!产生了显著影响。尽管[chosen_leader_lost_patron.GetHeShe]依旧蕴含强大的灵能能量，却失去了昔日由旧主庇护在[chosen_leader_lost_patron.GetHimHer]躯体中赋予的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:873`

### English

The event had a noticeable effect on §H[chosen_leader_lost_patron.GetName]§!. Though still imbued with considerable psionic energy, [chosen_leader_lost_patron.GetHeShe] [chosen_leader_lost_patron.GetHasHave] lost the power once bestowed in [chosen_leader_lost_patron.GetHimHer] by our former patron.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:937`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2686.name&lang=en)

### 中文

赐福武备

### English

Blessed Armaments

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:941`

## utopia.2686.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2686.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能使密会在研究如何利用超主§H[GetShroudPatron]§!的力量方面取得了突破性进展。在从前，只有激发了灵能的个体可以完全领悟契约的内涵，我们已经开发了一种能让这股力量贯穿整艘星舰的方法，这是此前从未达到过的程度。\n\n只要我们想，§H[GetShroudPatron]§!的力量能将我们的舰船推进得比以往更远。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:877`

### English

The Conclave of Telepaths has made a breakthrough in harnessing the powers of our patron, the §H[GetShroudPatron]§!. Where previously only psionic individuals could fully grasp the extent of the Covenant, we have devised a way to let power flow through entire starships, at a scale never seen before.\n\nShould we wish it, the mark of the §H[GetShroudPatron]§! can push our ships further than ever before.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:942`

## utopia.2686.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.2686.a&lang=en)

### English

## building_composer_sanctum

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_composer_sanctum&lang=en)

### 中文

织缕圣所

### English

Sanctum of the Composer

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:948`

## building_composer_sanctum_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_composer_sanctum_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

圣所是专门用于同生命之织缕进行沟通的地方。[GetComposerSanctumDesc]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:883`

### English

The Sanctum is a place dedicated to communion with the Composer. [GetComposerSanctumDesc]

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:949`

## building_composer_sanctum_desc_default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_composer_sanctum_desc_default&lang=en)

### 中文

在此，血肉如思绪一般狂放不羁，虚境能以远超我们的能力重塑我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:884`

### English

Here, flesh roams as freely as thought, allowing the Shroud to remake us beyond our means.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:950`

## building_composer_sanctum_desc_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_composer_sanctum_desc_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

在此，电路运行不受物理因素的阻碍。虚境能够以超越传统参数的方式优化我们的处理器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:885`

### English

Here, circuitry runs unimpeded by physical factors. The Shroud is able to optimize our processors in ways beyond our conventional parameters.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:951`

## building_eater_sanctum

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_eater_sanctum&lang=en)

### 中文

喰煞圣所

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:886`

### English

Sanctum of the Eater

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:952`

## building_eater_sanctum_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_eater_sanctum_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

圣所是专门用于同世界之喰煞进行沟通的地方。在此地，虚境为我们准备下一场战争，如同我们准备下一座[GetEaterSanctumDesc]一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:887`

### English

The Sanctum is a place dedicated to communion with the Eater. Here, the Shroud prepares us for war as we plan for the next [GetEaterSanctumDesc].

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:953`

## utopia.3400.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3400.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天选之帝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:920`

### English

A Divine Sovereign

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:990`

## utopia.3400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管曾是普通的[chosen_one_species.GetName]而今却已不可同日而语，这位名为§H[chosen_one.GetName]§!的[GetImmortalLoc]实体在[Root.GetName]吸引了大量虔诚的信徒。很多人都把他奉为至高神明，我们的政府也已经正式授予他[chosen_one.GetTitle]的称号，并成为了我们伟大帝国永远的统治者，从现在直到永远。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:921`

### English

The [GetImmortalLoc] entity known as §H[chosen_one.GetName]§!, previously [chosen_one_species.GetName] but now something far more, has attracted a large and devout following in the [Root.GetName]. Worshipped by many as a supreme deity, a call has gone out to officially crown the [chosen_one.GetTitle] as the permanent ruler of our great empire, now and into perpetuity.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:991`

## utopia.3400.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3400.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是个强大的灵能者，但是说是神？这就不太可信了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:922`

### English

A powerful psychic, but a God? Hardly.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:992`

## utopia.3400.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3400.b&lang=en)

### 中文

陛下千古一帝，万寿无疆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:923`

### English

May [chosen_one.GetHerHis] reign stretch into infinity... and beyond!

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:993`

[chosen_one_species.GetAdj][GetPsychicNoun]的强大力量夺取了§H[From.GetName]§!的控制！这个

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:926`

An immensely powerful [chosen_one_species.GetAdj] [GetPsychicNoun] has seized control of the §H[From.GetName]§!! This

## utopia.3401.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Autopia.3401.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

生物的追随者们悄悄地称[chosen_one.GetHerHis]为“天选之人”，今天早上在[From.Capital.GetName]上举行的一场奢华的仪式上加冕登基。随后，所有星际政府都收到了一封庆祝§H[chosen_one.GetRulerTitle][chosen_one.GetRegnalName]§!永恒统治的公报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/utopia_ascension_l_simp_chinese.yml:927`

### English

being, referred to in hushed whispers as the 'Chosen One' by [chosen_one.GetHerHis] followers, was crowned on [From.Capital.GetName] in a lavish ceremony this morning. A communique celebrating the eternal rule of §H[chosen_one.GetRulerTitle] [chosen_one.GetRegnalName]§! was sent out to all interstellar governments shortly thereafter.

Source: `localisation/english/utopia_ascension_l_english.yml:997`
